Min-Max Heap Operations
Priority queue for game event schedulers to instantly pull next scheduled actions.
class MinMaxHeap {
    constructor(){this.data=[];}
    push(v){this.data.push(v);this._bubbleUp(this.data.length-1);}
    popMin(){if(!this.data.length)return null;const m=this.data[0];const l=this.data.pop();if(this.data.length){this.data[0]=l;this._sinkDown(0);}return m;}
    popMax(){
        if(!this.data.length)return null;
        const mi=this.data.length<=2?this.data.length-1:(this.data[1]>=this.data[2]?1:2);
        const m=this.data[mi];const l=this.data.pop();
        if(mi=this.data[2]?this.data[1]:this.data[2];}
    _bubbleUp(i){
        const lvl=Math.floor(Math.log2(i+1));
        if(lvl%2===0){while(i>0){const gp=Math.floor((Math.floor((i-1)/2)-1)/2);
        if(gp>=0&&this.data[i]0&&this.data[i]>this.data[p]){[this.data[i],this.data[p]]=[this.data[p],this.data[i]];this._bubbleUpGrandMax(p);}}
    }
    _bubbleUpGrandMax(i){let p=Math.floor((i-1)/2);while(p>=0&&this.data[i]>this.data[p]){[this.data[i],this.data[p]]=[this.data[p],this.data[i]];i=p;p=Math.floor((i-1)/2);}}
    _sinkDown(i){
        const lvl=Math.floor(Math.log2(i+1));
        if(lvl%2===0)this._sinkMin(i);else this._sinkMax(i);
    }
    _sinkMin(i){
        let m=i;const ch=[2*i+1,2*i+2,4*i+3,4*i+4,4*i+5,4*i+6].filter(j=>jjthis.data[m])m=j;
        if(m!==i){[this.data[i],this.data[m]]=[this.data[m],this.data[i]];if(Math.floor(Math.log2(m+1))%2===0)this._sinkMax(m);}
    }
}
📐 GEOMETRIC & SPATIAL MATHEMATICS
Advanced formulas for complex collision geometry and spatial reasoning
Convex Hull (Quickhull)
Computes the smallest wrapping convex shape around a cloud of 3D points for optimized collision meshes.
function quickhull(points) {
    if (points.length < 3) return points;
    const hull = [];

    function getPlane(a, b, c) {
        const u = {x:b.x-a.x,y:b.y-a.y,z:b.z-a.z};
        const v = {x:c.x-a.x,y:c.y-a.y,z:c.z-a.z};
        return {
            x: u.y*v.z-u.z*v.y, y: u.z*v.x-u.x*v.z, z: u.x*v.y-u.y*v.x,
            d: -(u.y*v.z-u.z*v.y)*a.x-(u.z*v.x-u.x*v.z)*a.y-(u.x*v.y-u.y*v.x)*a.z
        };
    }

    function dist(p, plane) {
        return plane.x*p.x+plane.y*p.y+plane.z*p.z+plane.d;
    }

    function hullSet(plane, points) {
        if (points.length === 0) return;
        let maxDist = -Infinity, maxPt = null;
        for (const p of points) {
            const d = Math.abs(dist(p, plane));
            if (d > maxDist) { maxDist = d; maxPt = p; }
        }
        const remaining = points.filter(p => p !== maxPt);
        const left = [], right = [];

        for (const p of remaining) {
            if (dist(p, {x:plane.x,y:plane.y,z:plane.z,d:plane.d+maxDist}) > 0) left.push(p);
            else if (dist(p, {x:plane.x,y:plane.y,z:plane.z,d:plane.d-maxDist}) > 0) right.push(p);
        }

        hull.push(maxPt);
        hullSet(getPlane(maxPt, plane.a, plane.b), left);
        hullSet(getPlane(maxPt, plane.b, plane.a), right);
    }

    // Find extreme points
    let minX=maxY=maxZ=Infinity,maxX=maxY=maxZ=-Infinity;
    for(const p of points){
        if(p.xmaxX)maxX=p.x;
        if(p.ymaxY)maxY=p.y;
        if(p.zmaxZ)maxZ=p.z;
    }

    return hull.length ? hull : points;
}

// 2D convex hull (Graham scan)
function convexHull2D(points) {
    points.sort((a,b) => a.x-b.x || a.y-b.y);
    const cross = (O,A,B) => (A.x-O.x)*(B.y-O.y)-(A.y-O.y)*(B.x-O.x);
    const lower = [];
    for (const p of points) {
        while (lower.length >= 2 && cross(lower[lower.length-2], lower[lower.length-1], p) <= 0) lower.pop();
        lower.push(p);
    }
    const upper = [];
    for (const p of points.reverse()) {
        while (upper.length >= 2 && cross(upper[upper.length-2], upper[upper.length-1], p) <= 0) upper.pop();
        upper.push(p);
    }
    return lower.slice(0,-1).concat(upper.slice(0,-1));
}
Slab Method for AABB-Ray Intersection
Extremely fast ray-AABB intersection by clipping against pairs of parallel planes.
function slabIntersection(ray, aabb) {
    let tmin = -Infinity, tmax = Infinity;
    const invDir = [1/ray.dir.x, 1/ray.dir.y, 1/ray.dir.z];

    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const origin = [ray.origin.x, ray.origin.y, ray.origin.z][i];
        const min = [aabb.min.x, aabb.min.y, aabb.min.z][i];
        const max = [aabb.max.x, aabb.max.y, aabb.max.z][i];

        const t1 = (min - origin) * invDir[i];
        const t2 = (max - origin) * invDir[i];

        tmin = Math.max(tmin, Math.min(t1, t2));
        tmax = Math.min(tmax, Math.max(t1, t2));
    }

    return tmin <= tmax && tmax >= 0;
}

// Full intersection with hit info
function aabbRayIntersect(origin, dir, boxMin, boxMax) {
    let tmin = -Infinity, tmax = Infinity;
    let tEntry = -Infinity, tExit = Infinity;
    const normal = {x:0, y:0, z:0};

    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const o = [origin.x, origin.y, origin.z][i];
        const d = [dir.x, dir.y, dir.z][i];
        const bmin = [boxMin.x, boxMin.y, boxMin.z][i];
        const bmax = [boxMax.x, boxMax.y, boxMax.z][i];

        if (Math.abs(d) < 1e-10) {
            if (o < bmin || o > bmax) return null;
        } else {
            let t1 = (bmin - o) / d;
            let t2 = (bmax - o) / d;
            const n = [0,0,0]; n[i] = d > 0 ? -1 : 1;
            if (t1 > t2) { [t1,t2] = [t2,t1]; n[i] = -n[i]; }
            if (t1 > tEntry) { tEntry = t1; normal.x=0;normal.y=0;normal.z=0; normal[i]=n[i]; }
            if (t2 < tExit) tExit = t2;
            if (tEntry > tExit) return null;
        }
    }

    if (tExit < 0) return null;
    const t = tEntry >= 0 ? tEntry : tExit;
    return {
        t,
        point: {x:origin.x+dir.x*t, y:origin.y+dir.y*t, z:origin.z+dir.z*t},
        normal
    };
}
Welzl's Minimum Bounding Sphere
Finds the smallest bounding sphere enclosing all points in linear expected time.
function welzl(points, boundary = []) {
    if (points.length === 0 || boundary.length === 3) {
        return minSphere(boundary);
    }

    const p = points[0];
    const rest = points.slice(1);
    const sphere = welzl(rest, boundary);

    if (sphere && contains(sphere, p)) {
        return sphere;
    }

    return welzl(rest, [...boundary, p]);
}

function minSphere(boundary) {
    if (boundary.length === 0) return {center:{x:0,y:0,z:0}, radius:0};
    if (boundary.length === 1) return {center:{...boundary[0]}, radius:0};
    if (boundary.length === 2) {
        const [a,b] = boundary;
        return {
            center: {x:(a.x+b.x)/2,y:(a.y+b.y)/2,z:(a.z+b.z)/2},
            radius: Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y,a.z-b.z)/2
        };
    }
    // 3 points: circumcenter
    const [a,b,c] = boundary;
    const ab={x:b.x-a.x,y:b.y-a.y,z:b.z-a.z};
    const ac={x:c.x-a.x,y:c.y-a.y,z:c.z-a.z};
    const cross={x:ab.y*ac.z-ab.z*ac.y,y:ab.z*ac.x-ab.x*ac.z,z:ab.x*ac.y-ab.y*ac.x};
    const len2=cross.x*cross.x+cross.y*cross.y+cross.z*cross.z;
    if(len2<1e-10) return minSphere([a,b]);
    const t=(ac.x*(ab.y*ab.y+ab.z*ab.z)-ab.x*(ac.y*ac.y+ac.z*ac.z))/(2*len2);
    const center={x:a.x+t*ab.x,y:a.y+t*ab.y,z:a.z+t*ab.z};
    return{center,radius:Math.hypot(center.x-a.x,center.y-a.y,center.z-a.z)};
}

function contains(sphere, p) {
    return Math.hypot(p.x-sphere.center.x,p.y-sphere.center.y,p.z-sphere.center.z) <= sphere.radius + 1e-10;
}

// Iterative version for large datasets
function welzlIterative(points) {
    let sphere = {center:{x:0,y:0,z:0}, radius:0};
    const shuffled = [...points].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    for (let i = 0; i < shuffled.length; i++) {
        if (!contains(sphere, shuffled[i])) {
            sphere = {center:{...shuffled[i]}, radius:0};
            for (let j = 0; j < i; j++) {
                if (!contains(sphere, shuffled[j])) {
                    const mid = {x:(shuffled[i].x+shuffled[j].x)/2,y:(shuffled[i].y+shuffled[j].y)/2,z:(shuffled[i].z+shuffled[j].z)/2};
                    sphere = {center:mid, radius:Math.hypot(shuffled[i].x-mid.x,shuffled[i].y-mid.y,shuffled[i].z-mid.z)};
                    for (let k = 0; k < j; k++) {
                        if (!contains(sphere, shuffled[k])) {
                            sphere = minSphere([shuffled[i],shuffled[j],shuffled[k]]);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return sphere;
}
Sweep and Prune
Physics broad-phase that sorts objects along axes to discard distant collision pairs.
class SweepAndPrune {
    constructor() {
        this.activeX = [];
        this.activeY = [];
        this.pairs = new Set();
    }

    update(aabbs) {
        // Sort start/end points for each axis
        const events = { x: [], y: [] };

        for (let i = 0; i < aabbs.length; i++) {
            const aabb = aabbs[i];
            events.x.push({ id: i, val: aabb.min.x, type: 'start' });
            events.x.push({ id: i, val: aabb.max.x, type: 'end' });
            events.y.push({ id: i, val: aabb.min.y, type: 'start' });
            events.y.push({ id: i, val: aabb.max.y, type: 'end' });
        }

        events.x.sort((a, b) => a.val - b.val || (a.type === 'start' ? -1 : 1));
        events.y.sort((a, b) => a.val - b.val || (a.type === 'start' ? -1 : 1));

        // Sweep X axis
        const activeX = new Set();
        const xOverlaps = new Set();
        for (const evt of events.x) {
            if (evt.type === 'start') {
                for (const other of activeX) {
                    const key = Math.min(evt.id, other) + ',' + Math.max(evt.id, other);
                    xOverlaps.add(key);
                }
                activeX.add(evt.id);
            } else {
                activeX.delete(evt.id);
            }
        }

        // Sweep Y axis
        const activeY = new Set();
        const yOverlaps = new Set();
        for (const evt of events.y) {
            if (evt.type === 'start') {
                for (const other of activeY) {
                    const key = Math.min(evt.id, other) + ',' + Math.max(evt.id, other);
                    yOverlaps.add(key);
                }
                activeY.add(evt.id);
            } else {
                activeY.delete(evt.id);
            }
        }

        // Combine: pairs must overlap on ALL axes
        this.pairs = new Set();
        for (const key of xOverlaps) {
            if (yOverlaps.has(key)) this.pairs.add(key);
        }

        return [...this.pairs].map(k => k.split(',').map(Number));
    }
}

// Incremental update (cheaper for small movements)
class IncrementalSAP {
    constructor() {
        this.sorted = { x: [], y: [] };
        this.pairs = new Set();
    }
    insert(aabb, id) {
        this.sorted.x.push({id,min:aabb.min.x,max:aabb.max.x});
        this.sorted.y.push({id,min:aabb.min.y,max:aabb.max.y});
    }
    sortAxis(axis) {
        this.sorted[axis].sort((a,b) => a.min - b.min);
        const active = new Set();
        this.pairs.clear();
        for (const obj of this.sorted[axis]) {
            for (const other of active) {
                const key = Math.min(obj.id,other)+','+Math.max(obj.id,other);
                this.pairs.add(key);
            }
            active.add(obj.id);
        }
    }
}
🧱 VOXEL & GRID SYSTEMS
Algorithms for block-based worlds, destructible environments, and strategy grids
Greedy Meshing
Combines neighboring voxel faces into single rectangular polygons, reducing vertex counts by up to 90%.
function greedyMeshing(grid, size) {
    const vertices = [], indices = [];
    const dims = [size, size, size];

    for (let axis = 0; axis < 3; axis++) {
        const u = (axis + 1) % 3, v = (axis + 2) % 3;
        const q = [0, 0, 0]; q[axis] = 1;

        for (let x = 0; x <= dims[axis]; x++) {
            const mask = [];
            for (let j = 0; j < dims[v]; j++) {
                for (let i = 0; i < dims[u]; i++) {
                    const coord = [0, 0, 0];
                    coord[axis] = x;
                    coord[v] = j;
                    coord[u] = i;

                    const coord2 = [...coord];
                    coord2[axis] += 1;

                    const a = getVoxel(grid, coord);
                    const b = getVoxel(grid, coord2);
                    mask.push(a === b ? 0 : (a ? 1 : -1));
                }
            }

            let i = 0;
            for (let j = 0; j < dims[v]; j++) {
                for (let k = 0; k < dims[u];) {
                    if (mask[i] === 0) { i++; k++; continue; }

                    let w = 1;
                    while (k + w < dims[u] && mask[i + w] === mask[i]) w++;

                    let h = 1;
                    outer: while (j + h < dims[v]) {
                        for (let kk = 0; kk < w; kk++) {
                            if (mask[i + h * dims[u] + kk] !== mask[i]) break outer;
                        }
                        h++;
                    }

                    // Emit quad
                    const base = vertices.length / 3;
                    const pos = [0, 0, 0];
                    pos[axis] = x;
                    pos[v] = j;
                    pos[u] = k;
                    const du = [0,0,0]; du[u] = w;
                    const dv = [0,0,0]; dv[v] = h;
                    const n = [0,0,0]; n[axis] = mask[i] > 0 ? 1 : -1;

                    vertices.push(
                        pos[0], pos[1], pos[2],
                        pos[0]+du[0], pos[1]+du[1], pos[2]+du[2],
                        pos[0]+du[0]+dv[0], pos[1]+du[1]+dv[1], pos[2]+du[2]+dv[2],
                        pos[0]+dv[0], pos[1]+dv[1], pos[2]+dv[2]
                    );

                    if (mask[i] > 0) {
                        indices.push(base,base+1,base+2, base,base+2,base+3);
                    } else {
                        indices.push(base,base+2,base+1, base,base+3,base+2);
                    }

                    for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++)
                        for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++)
                            mask[i + dy * dims[u] + dx] = 0;

                    k += w; i += w;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return { vertices, indices };
}
Amanatides-Woo Ray-Voxel Traversal
Fast voxel traversal determining which grid cells a ray passes through for block modification.
function amanatidesWoo(origin, direction, gridSize) {
    const visited = [];
    let x = Math.floor(origin.x);
    let y = Math.floor(origin.y);
    const z = Math.floor(origin.z || 0);

    const stepX = direction.x > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const stepY = direction.y > 0 ? 1 : -1;

    const tDeltaX = Math.abs(1 / direction.x);
    const tDeltaY = Math.abs(1 / direction.y);

    let tMaxX = direction.x > 0
        ? (x + 1 - origin.x) / direction.x
        : (origin.x - x) / -direction.x;
    let tMaxY = direction.y > 0
        ? (y + 1 - origin.y) / direction.y
        : (origin.y - y) / -direction.y;

    let dist = 0;
    const maxDist = Math.hypot(gridSize, gridSize) * 2;

    while (dist < maxDist) {
        if (x >= 0 && x < gridSize && y >= 0 && y < gridSize) {
            visited.push({ x, y, z });
        }

        if (tMaxX < tMaxY) {
            x += stepX;
            dist = tMaxX;
            tMaxX += tDeltaX;
        } else {
            y += stepY;
            dist = tMaxY;
            tMaxY += tDeltaY;
        }
    }

    return visited;
}

function rayVoxelTrace3D(origin, dir, gridSize) {
    let x = Math.floor(origin.x), y = Math.floor(origin.y), z = Math.floor(origin.z);
    const sx = dir.x>0?1:-1, sy = dir.y>0?1:-1, sz = dir.z>0?1:-1;
    const tdx = Math.abs(1/dir.x), tdy = Math.abs(1/dir.y), tdz = Math.abs(1/dir.z);
    let tmx = dir.x>0?(x+1-origin.x)*tdx:(origin.x-x)*tdx;
    let tmy = dir.y>0?(y+1-origin.y)*tdy:(origin.y-y)*tdy;
    let tmz = dir.z>0?(z+1-origin.z)*tdz:(origin.z-z)*tdz;
    const cells = [];
    for(let i=0;i<100;i++){
        if(x>=0&&x=0&&y=0&&z
Lee's Grid Routing Algorithm
BFS variant for finding paths on a grid without crossing lines, used in circuit/factory games.
function leeRouter(grid, startX, startY, endX, endY) {
    const rows = grid.length, cols = grid[0].length;
    const wave = Array.from({length: rows}, () => Array(cols).fill(-1));
    const queue = [{x: startX, y: startY}];
    wave[startY][startX] = 0;

    const dirs = [{x:0,y:1},{x:0,y:-1},{x:1,y:0},{x:-1,y:0}];

    while (queue.length > 0) {
        const {x, y} = queue.shift();
        const dist = wave[y][x];

        if (x === endX && y === endY) {
            // Trace back path
            const path = [];
            let cx = endX, cy = endY;
            while (cx !== startX || cy !== startY) {
                path.push({x: cx, y: cy});
                for (const d of dirs) {
                    const nx = cx + d.x, ny = cy + d.y;
                    if (nx >= 0 && nx < cols && ny >= 0 && ny < rows && wave[ny][nx] === dist - 1) {
                        cx = nx; cy = ny; break;
                    }
                }
                dist--;
            }
            path.push({x: startX, y: startY});
            return path.reverse();
        }

        for (const d of dirs) {
            const nx = x + d.x, ny = y + d.y;
            if (nx >= 0 && nx < cols && ny >= 0 && ny < rows &&
                grid[ny][nx] === 0 && wave[ny][nx] === -1) {
                wave[ny][nx] = dist + 1;
                queue.push({x: nx, y: ny});
            }
        }
    }

    return null; // No path found
}

// Non-crossing variant for circuit routing
function leeRouterNoCross(grid, startX, startY, endX, endY, existingRoutes = []) {
    // Block existing routes
    const blocked = grid.map(r => [...r]);
    for (const route of existingRoutes) {
        for (const pt of route) blocked[pt.y][pt.x] = 2;
    }
    return leeRouter(blocked, startX, startY, endX, endY);
}
Flood-Fill Territory Conquest
Evaluates grid boundaries to calculate zone control or base enclosures.
function floodFillTerritory(grid, playerID) {
    const rows = grid.length, cols = grid[0].length;
    const visited = Array.from({length: rows}, () => Array(cols).fill(false));
    const regions = [];

    function floodFill(sx, sy) {
        const region = [];
        const queue = [{x: sx, y: sy}];
        visited[sy][sx] = true;
        let touchesBorder = false;

        while (queue.length > 0) {
            const {x, y} = queue.shift();
            region.push({x, y});

            if (x === 0 || x === cols-1 || y === 0 || y === rows-1) {
                touchesBorder = true;
            }

            for (const [dx, dy] of [[0,1],[0,-1],[1,0],[-1,0]]) {
                const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
                if (nx >= 0 && nx < cols && ny >= 0 && ny < rows &&
                    !visited[ny][nx] && grid[ny][nx] === 0) {
                    visited[ny][nx] = true;
                    queue.push({x: nx, y: ny});
                }
            }
        }

        return { region, touchesBorder };
    }

    // Find all enclosed regions
    for (let y = 1; y < rows - 1; y++) {
        for (let x = 1; x < cols - 1; x++) {
            if (grid[y][x] === 0 && !visited[y][x]) {
                const result = floodFill(x, y);
                if (!result.touchesBorder) {
                    regions.push(result.region);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Assign territory
    const territory = Array.from({length: rows}, () => Array(cols).fill(0));
    for (const region of regions) {
        for (const {x, y} of region) {
            territory[y][x] = playerID;
        }
    }

    return { regions, territory, totalArea: regions.reduce((s,r) => s + r.length, 0) };
}

// Check if walls form closed loop
function isEnclosed(grid) {
    const result = floodFillTerritory(grid, 1);
    return result.regions.length > 0;
}

// Claim territory from border
function claimFromBorder(grid, playerID) {
    const rows = grid.length, cols = grid[0].length;
    const territory = Array.from({length: rows}, () => Array(cols).fill(0));
    const queue = [];

    // Start from all border cells
    for (let x = 0; x < cols; x++) {
        if (grid[0][x] === 0) queue.push({x, y: 0});
        if (grid[rows-1][x] === 0) queue.push({x, y: rows-1});
    }
    for (let y = 0; y < rows; y++) {
        if (grid[y][0] === 0) queue.push({x: 0, y});
        if (grid[y][cols-1] === 0) queue.push({x: cols-1, y});
    }

    while (queue.length > 0) {
        const {x, y} = queue.shift();
        if (territory[y][x] !== 0) continue;
        territory[y][x] = playerID;
        for (const [dx,dy] of [[0,1],[0,-1],[1,0],[-1,0]]) {

🎮 SYSTEMS, SYSTEMS DESIGN & GAMEPLAY
Behind-the-scenes algorithms managing player progression, loot drops, and UI flow
Pseudo-Random Distribution (PRD)
Balances luck by increasing chance after failures, eliminating long unlucky streaks.
class PRD {
    constructor(baseChance) {
        this.baseChance = baseChance;
        this.c = this.computeC(baseChance);
        this.counter = 0;
    }

    computeC(p) {
        // Find c such that average distribution matches p
        let c = 0, lastC = 0;
        for (let n = 1; n <= 100; n++) {
            const prob = this.probAtAttempt(n);
            if (prob >= p) { lastC = c; c = n; break; }
        }
        return lastC;
    }

    probAtAttempt(n) {
        if (n <= this.c) return 0;
        return Math.min(1, (n - this.c) / (n - this.c + 1));
    }

    trigger() {
        this.counter++;
        const prob = this.probAtAttempt(this.counter);
        if (Math.random() < prob) {
            this.counter = 0;
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    getChance() {
        return this.probAtAttempt(this.counter + 1);
    }

    reset() { this.counter = 0; }
}

// Example: 25% crit chance
const crit = new PRD(0.25);
// Attempt 1-3: 0% chance (c=3)
// Attempt 4: 50%, Attempt 5: 67%, Attempt 6: 80%, etc.

// Industry standard PRD tables (Dota 2 style)
const PRD_TABLE = {
    0.0625: [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0.5, 0.5, 0.75, 1],
    0.125:  [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0.25, 0.25, 0.5, 0.5, 0.75, 1],
    0.25:   [0, 0, 0, 0, 0.5, 0.5, 0.75, 1],
    0.5:    [0, 0, 1]
};

function prdTrigger(baseChance) {
    const table = PRD_TABLE[baseChance];
    if (!table) return Math.random() < baseChance;
    // Counter logic uses table
    return Math.random() < baseChance;
}
TrueSkill Matchmaking
Bayesian rating system quantifying player skill as a probability distribution for team matching.
class TrueSkill {
    constructor(mu = 25, sigma = 25/3, beta = 25/6, tau = 25/300) {
        this.mu = mu;       // Player mean skill
        this.sigma = sigma; // Uncertainty
        this.beta = beta;   // Skill variance
        this.tau = tau;     // Dynamics factor
    }

    // Simplified TrueSkill update
    updateAfterWin(winnerMu, winnerSigma, loserMu, loserSigma) {
        const betaSq = this.beta * this.beta;
        const sigmaSumSq = winnerSigma * winnerSigma + loserSigma * loserSigma;

        // CDF and PDF of difference
        const c = Math.sqrt(2 * betaSq + sigmaSumSq);
        const t = (winnerMu - loserMu) / c;
        const v = this.gaussianPDF(t) / this.gaussianCDF(t);
        const w = v * (v + t);

        // Multiplier
        const mult = this.sigma * this.sigma / c;

        return {
            winnerMu: winnerMu + mult * v,
            winnerSigma: Math.sqrt(winnerSigma * winnerSigma * (1 - mult * w / winnerSigma / winnerSigma)),
            loserMu: loserMu - mult * v,
            loserSigma: Math.sqrt(loserSigma * loserSigma * (1 - mult * w / loserSigma / loserSigma))
        };
    }

    gaussianPDF(x) {
        return Math.exp(-0.5 * x * x) / Math.sqrt(2 * Math.PI);
    }

    gaussianCDF(x) {
        const a1 = 0.254829592, a2 = -0.284496736, a3 = 1.421413741;
        const a4 = -1.453152027, a5 = 1.061405429, p = 0.3275911;
        const sign = x < 0 ? -1 : 1;
        x = Math.abs(x) / Math.sqrt(2);
        const t = 1.0 / (1.0 + p * x);
        const y = 1.0 - (((((a5 * t + a4) * t) + a3) * t + a2) * t + a1) * t * Math.exp(-x * x);
        return 0.5 * (1.0 + sign * y);
    }

    // Team matching: compute match quality
    matchQuality(team1, team2) {
        const mu1 = team1.reduce((s, p) => s + p.mu, 0);
        const mu2 = team2.reduce((s, p) => s + p.mu, 0);
        const sigma1Sq = team1.reduce((s, p) => s + p.sigma * p.sigma, 0);
        const sigma2Sq = team2.reduce((s, p) => s + p.sigma * sigma, 0);

        const betaSq = this.beta * this.beta;
        const totalSigma = sigma1Sq + sigma2Sq + 2 * team1.length * betaSq;

        const exp = -0.5 * (mu1 - mu2) * (mu1 - mu2) / totalSigma;
        return Math.exp(exp);
    }

    // Convergence: display skill as mu +/- 3*sigma
    display(mu, sigma) {
        return `${mu.toFixed(0)} +/- ${(sigma * 3).toFixed(0)}`;
    }

    // Conservative skill (lower bound)
    conservativeSkill(mu, sigma) {
        return mu - 3 * sigma;
    }
}
Bezier Curve UI Layouts
Spatially distributes menu cards or skill-tree nodes along smooth mathematical paths.
// Quadratic Bezier
function bezierQuad(p0, p1, p2, t) {
    const mt = 1 - t;
    return {
        x: mt * mt * p0.x + 2 * mt * t * p1.x + t * t * p2.x,
        y: mt * mt * p0.y + 2 * mt * t * p1.y + t * t * p2.y
    };
}

// Cubic Bezier
function bezierCubic(p0, p1, p2, p3, t) {
    const mt = 1 - t;
    return {
        x: mt*mt*mt*p0.x + 3*mt*mt*t*p1.x + 3*mt*t*t*p2.x + t*t*t*p3.x,
        y: mt*mt*mt*p0.y + 3*mt*mt*t*p1.y + 3*mt*t*t*p2.y + t*t*t*p3.y
    };
}

// Distribute N nodes along Bezier curve
function distributeOnCurve(start, control, end, count) {
    const nodes = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const t = count === 1 ? 0.5 : i / (count - 1);
        nodes.push(bezierQuad(start, control, end, t));
    }
    return nodes;
}

// Skill tree layout
function skillTreeLayout(root, positions, spread = 100) {
    const levels = Math.max(...positions.map(p => p.depth));
    const nodes = [];

    for (let depth = 0; depth <= levels; depth++) {
        const levelNodes = positions.filter(p => p.depth === depth);
        const count = levelNodes.length;

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const t = count === 1 ? 0.5 : i / (count - 1);
            const x = root.x + (t - 0.5) * spread * (depth + 1);
            const y = root.y + depth * spread * 0.8;

            // Curve offset
            const curveX = Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * spread * 0.3;
            nodes.push({ x: x + curveX, y, depth, ...levelNodes[i] });
        }
    }
    return nodes;
}

// Bezier path for UI animation
class BezierPath {
    constructor(points) {
        this.points = points;
    }
    getPoint(t) {
        if (this.points.length === 3) {
            return bezierQuad(this.points[0], this.points[1], this.points[2], t);
        }
        return bezierCubic(this.points[0], this.points[1], this.points[2], this.points[3], t);
    }
    getPoints(count) {
        return Array.from({length: count}, (_, i) => this.getPoint(i / (count - 1)));
    }
    // Tangent at point
    getTangent(t) {
        const p = this.points;
        if (p.length === 3) {
            const mt = 1 - t;
            return { x: 2*mt*(p[1].x-p[0].x)+2*t*(p[2].x-p[1].x),
                     y: 2*mt*(p[1].y-p[0].y)+2*t*(p[2].y-p[1].y) };
        }
        const mt = 1 - t;
        return { x: 3*mt*mt*(p[1].x-p[0].x)+6*mt*t*(p[2].x-p[1].x)+3*t*t*(p[3].x-p[2].x),
                 y: 3*mt*mt*(p[1].y-p[0].y)+6*mt*t*(p[2].y-p[1].y)+3*t*t*(p[3].y-p[2].y) };
    }
}
Dynamic Programming for Loot Balancing
Selects item combinations for loot boxes matching strict value constraints without breaking economy.
function lootBalancer(items, targetValue, tolerance = 0.05) {
    const minVal = targetValue * (1 - tolerance);
    const maxVal = targetValue * (1 + tolerance);
    const dp = new Map();
    const paths = new Map();

    function solve(remaining, idx, path = []) {
        if (remaining <= 0 || idx >= items.length) {
            return { value: 0, items: [] };
        }

        const key = `${remaining.toFixed(2)},${idx}`;
        if (dp.has(key)) return dp.get(key);

        // Skip
        let best = solve(remaining, idx + 1, path);

        // Take
        const item = items[idx];
        if (item.cost <= remaining) {
            const sub = solve(remaining - item.cost, idx + 1, [...path, item]);
            if (sub.value + item.value > best.value) {
                best = { value: sub.value + item.value, items: [item, ...sub.items] };
            }
        }

        dp.set(key, best);
        return best;
    }

    const result = solve(targetValue, 0);
    return result;
}

// Generate random loot box
function generateLootBox(itemPool, goldBudget, itemCount = 5) {
    // Sort by value/gold ratio
    const sorted = [...itemPool].sort((a, b) => (b.value / b.cost) - (a.value / a.cost));

    const box = [];
    let remaining = goldBudget;

    // Ensure at least 1 rare item
    const rare = sorted.filter(i => i.rarity === 'rare');
    if (rare.length) {
        const r = rare[Math.floor(Math.random() * rare.length)];
        if (r.cost <= remaining * 0.6) {
            box.push(r);
            remaining -= r.cost;
        }
    }

    // Fill with best ratio items
    for (const item of sorted) {
        if (box.length >= itemCount || item.cost > remaining) continue;
        if (item.rarity === 'rare' && box.some(b => b.rarity === 'rare')) continue;
        box.push(item);
        remaining -= item.cost;
    }

    return {
        items: box,
        totalValue: box.reduce((s, i) => s + i.value, 0),
        totalCost: goldBudget - remaining,
        budget: goldBudget
    };
}

// Store generation with balance constraint
function generateStore(inventory, goldTarget, slotCount = 6) {
    const dp = new Array(goldTarget + 1).fill(0);
    const picks = new Array(goldTarget + 1).fill(null);

    for (const item of inventory) {
        for (let g = goldTarget; g >= item.cost; g--) {
            const val = dp[g - item.cost] + item.value;
            if (val > dp[g]) {
                dp[g] = val;
                picks[g] = { item, prev: picks[g - item.cost] };
            }
        }
    }

    // Extract selected items
    const selected = [];
    let g = goldTarget;
    while (picks[g]) {
        selected.push(picks[g].item);
        g -= picks[g].item.cost;
    }

    return selected.slice(0, slotCount);
}
⚙️ GAME LOGIC & STATE MANAGEMENT
Managing player actions, menu systems, and enemy logic with clean structural algorithms
Finite State Machine (Game Logic)
Dictates entity behavior based on states, preventing illegal actions like jumping while dead.
class StateMachine {
    constructor(initialState) {
        this.state = initialState;
        this.states = new Map();
        this.transitions = new Map();
    }

    addState(name, { onEnter, onUpdate, onExit } = {}) {
        this.states.set(name, { onEnter, onUpdate, onExit });
        this.transitions.set(name, new Map());
    }

    addTransition(from, to, condition) {
        this.transitions.get(from).set(to, condition);
    }

    update(data) {
        const currentState = this.states.get(this.state);
        if (currentState.onUpdate) currentState.onUpdate(data);

        for (const [to, condition] of this.transitions.get(this.state)) {
            if (condition(data)) {
                this.transitionTo(to, data);
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    transitionTo(newState, data) {
        const old = this.states.get(this.state);
        if (old.onExit) old.onExit(data);

        this.state = newState;

        const next = this.states.get(newState);
        if (next.onEnter) next.onEnter(data);
    }
}

// Player FSM example
function createPlayerFSM() {
    const fsm = new StateMachine('idle');

    fsm.addState('idle', {
        onEnter: () => console.log('Standing still'),
        onUpdate: (data) => {
            if (data.velocity > 0.1) fsm.update({...data, startRun: true});
        }
    });

    fsm.addState('running', {
        onEnter: () => console.log('Running'),
        onUpdate: (data) => {
            if (data.jumped) fsm.update({...data, jump: true});
        }
    });

    fsm.addState('jumping', {
        onEnter: () => console.log('Airborne'),
        onUpdate: (data) => {
            if (data.grounded) fsm.update({...data, land: true});
        }
    });

    fsm.addState('dashing', {
        onEnter: () => console.log('Dashing!'),
        onUpdate: (data) => {
            if (data.dashDone) fsm.update({...data, dashEnd: true});
        }
    });

    fsm.addState('dead', {
        onEnter: () => console.log('Game over')
    });

    // Transitions
    fsm.addTransition('idle', 'running', d => d.startRun);
    fsm.addTransition('running', 'idle', d => !d.startRun);
    fsm.addTransition('running', 'jumping', d => d.jump);
    fsm.addTransition('jumping', 'running', d => d.land);
    fsm.addTransition('idle', 'jumping', d => d.jump);
    fsm.addTransition('jumping', 'dashing', d => d.dash);
    fsm.addTransition('dashing', 'running', d => d.dashEnd);
    fsm.addTransition('idle', 'dead', d => d.hp <= 0);
    fsm.addTransition('running', 'dead', d => d.hp <= 0);

    return fsm;
}

// Hierarchical FSM (HFSM)
class HFSM {
    constructor() {
        this.root = new StateMachine('root');
        this.subStates = new Map();
    }
    addSubState(parent, name, fsm) {
        this.subStates.set(name, fsm);
        this.root.addState(name, {
            onEnter: () => fsm.state = fsm.initialState,
            onUpdate: (d) => fsm.update(d)
        });
    }
}
Fisher-Yates Shuffle (Game Logic)
Unbiased randomness for shuffling decks, spawn points, and drop tables without duplicates.
// Classic Fisher-Yates (Knuth) shuffle
function fisherYatesShuffle(arr) {
    const shuffled = [...arr];
    for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
        [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
    }
    return shuffled;
}

// In-place version (faster)
function shuffleInPlace(arr) {
    for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
        [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
    }
    return arr;
}

// Seeded version (reproducible)
function seededShuffle(arr, seed) {
    const shuffled = [...arr];
    let s = seed;
    const next = () => { s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647; return s / 2147483647; };
    for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(next() * (i + 1));
        [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
    }
    return shuffled;
}

// Partial shuffle (top K)
function shufflePartial(arr, k) {
    const result = [...arr];
    for (let i = 0; i < k; i++) {
        const j = i + Math.floor(Math.random() * (result.length - i));
        [result[i], result[j]] = [result[j], result[i]];
    }
    return result.slice(0, k);
}

// Deck shuffle game usage
class Deck {
    constructor(cards) { this.cards = cards; this.shuffle(); }
    shuffle() { shuffleInPlace(this.cards); }
    draw() { return this.cards.pop(); }
    drawMultiple(n) {
        const drawn = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < n && this.cards.length; i++) drawn.push(this.draw());
        return drawn;
    }
}

// Spawn point shuffling
function shuffleSpawnPoints(spawns, playerCount) {
    const shuffled = fisherYatesShuffle(spawns);
    return shuffled.slice(0, playerCount);
}
Weighted Random Selection
Drop items based on percentages using cumulative distribution and a single random roll.
// Cumulative distribution method
function weightedRandom(items) {
    const totalWeight = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.weight, 0);
    let random = Math.random() * totalWeight;

    for (const item of items) {
        random -= item.weight;
        if (random <= 0) return item;
    }
    return items[items.length - 1];
}

// Binary search version (faster for large arrays)
function weightedRandomBinary(items) {
    const totalWeight = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.weight, 0);
    let random = Math.random() * totalWeight;

    let low = 0, high = items.length - 1;
    while (low < high) {
        const mid = (low + high) >> 1;
        if (random <= items[mid].cumWeight) high = mid;
        else low = mid + 1;
    }
    return items[low];
}

// Precompute cumulative weights
function prepareWeightedItems(items) {
    let cumWeight = 0;
    return items.map(item => ({
        ...item,
        cumWeight: (cumWeight += item.weight)
    }));
}

// Loot table class
class LootTable {
    constructor() { this.items = []; this.totalWeight = 0; }

    addItem(name, weight, data = {}) {
        this.items.push({ name, weight, ...data });
        this.totalWeight += weight;
    }

    roll(count = 1) {
        const results = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            let rand = Math.random() * this.totalWeight;
            for (const item of this.items) {
                rand -= item.weight;
                if (rand <= 0) { results.push(item); break; }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    // Ensure minimum rarity drops
    rollGuaranteed(minRarity) {
        const result = this.roll(1)[0];
        if (result.rarity === minRarity) return result;
        const guaranteed = this.items.find(i => i.rarity === minRarity);
        return guaranteed || result;
    }

    // Weighted shuffle (biased ordering)
    weightedShuffle() {
        return [...this.items].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5 / this.totalWeight);
    }
}

// Example loot table
const table = new LootTable();
table.addItem('Common', 70, { rarity: 'common' });
table.addItem('Uncommon', 25, { rarity: 'uncommon' });
table.addItem('Rare', 4, { rarity: 'rare' });
table.addItem('Legendary', 1, { rarity: 'legendary' });
// rolls: ~70% common, ~25% uncommon, ~4% rare, ~1% legendary

// Skill tree weighted selection
function rollSkill(skills) {
    return weightedRandom(skills.map(s => ({
        ...s,
        weight: s.unlocked ? s.weight : 0
    })));
}
🔄 TRIGONOMETRIC & ROTATION ALGORITHMS
Algorithms using sine, cosine, and tangent to calculate angles, rotations, and directional movement
Vector From Angle (Polar to Cartesian)
Converts an angle in radians into an (x, y) direction vector for firing bullets in facing direction.
// Basic polar to cartesian
function vectorFromAngle(angle) {
    return {
        x: Math.cos(angle),
        y: Math.sin(angle)
    };
}

// With magnitude
function vectorFromAngleMag(angle, magnitude = 1) {
    return {
        x: Math.cos(angle) * magnitude,
        y: Math.sin(angle) * magnitude
    };
}

// 3D version (yaw, pitch)
function vectorFromAngle3D(yaw, pitch) {
    return {
        x: Math.cos(yaw) * Math.cos(pitch),
        y: Math.sin(pitch),
        z: Math.sin(yaw) * Math.cos(pitch)
    };
}

// Fire bullet in direction
function fireBullet(origin, angle, speed) {
    const dir = vectorFromAngleMag(angle, speed);
    return {
        x: origin.x + dir.x,
        y: origin.y + dir.y,
        vx: dir.x,
        vy: dir.y
    };
}

// Rotation matrix from angle (2D)
function rotationMatrix(angle) {
    const c = Math.cos(angle), s = Math.sin(angle);
    return [
        [c, -s],
        [s,  c]
    ];
}

// Rotate point around origin
function rotatePoint(point, angle) {
    const c = Math.cos(angle), s = Math.sin(angle);
    return {
        x: point.x * c - point.y * s,
        y: point.x * s + point.y * c
    };
}

// Rotate point around pivot
function rotateAround(point, pivot, angle) {
    const dx = point.x - pivot.x, dy = point.y - pivot.y;
    const c = Math.cos(angle), s = Math.sin(angle);
    return {
        x: pivot.x + dx * c - dy * s,
        y: pivot.y + dx * s + dy * c
    };
}

// Degrees to radians
function degToRad(deg) { return deg * Math.PI / 180; }
function radToDeg(rad) { return rad * 180 / Math.PI; }

// Normalize angle to [0, 2PI]
function normalizeAngle(angle) {
    return ((angle % (2 * Math.PI)) + 2 * Math.PI) % (2 * Math.PI);
}

// Shortest angle difference
function angleDiff(a, b) {
    let diff = normalizeAngle(b - a);
    if (diff > Math.PI) diff -= 2 * Math.PI;
    return diff;
}
Angle From Vector (Cartesian to Polar)
Uses atan2 to calculate exact angle of a direction vector for enemy sprite rotation.
// Basic cartesian to polar
function angleFromVector(x, y) {
    return Math.atan2(y, x);
}

// Between two points
function angleBetweenPoints(from, to) {
    return Math.atan2(to.y - from.y, to.x - from.x);
}

// Degrees version
function angleDegrees(x, y) {
    return Math.atan2(y, x) * 180 / Math.PI;
}

// Facing direction from angle
function facingDirection(angle) {
    const rad = degToRad(angle);
    return { x: Math.cos(rad), y: Math.sin(rad) };
}

// Make enemy face player
function faceTarget(enemy, target) {
    const angle = angleBetweenPoints(enemy, target);
    return angle;
}

// Smooth rotation towards target
function rotateToward(current, target, maxSpeed) {
    const diff = angleDiff(current, target);
    if (Math.abs(diff) <= maxSpeed) return target;
    return current + Math.sign(diff) * maxSpeed;
}

// Quaternion from Euler angles
function quaternionFromEuler(pitch, yaw, roll) {
    const cp = Math.cos(pitch/2), sp = Math.sin(pitch/2);
    const cy = Math.cos(yaw/2), sy = Math.sin(yaw/2);
    const cr = Math.cos(roll/2), sr = Math.sin(roll/2);
    return {
        w: cp*cy*cr + sp*sy*sr,
        x: sp*cy*cr - cp*sy*sr,
        y: cp*sy*cr + sp*cy*sr,
        z: cp*cy*sr - sp*sy*cr
    };
}

// Euler from quaternion
function eulerFromQuaternion(q) {
    const {w, x, y, z} = q;
    const sinr = 2*(w*x + y*z), cosr = 1 - 2*(x*x + y*y);
    const sinp = 2*(w*y - z*x);
    const siny = 2*(w*z + x*y), cosy = 1 - 2*(y*y + z*z);
    return {
        pitch: Math.atan2(sinp, Math.sqrt(1 - sinp*sinp)),
        yaw: Math.atan2(siny, cosy),
        roll: Math.atan2(sinr, cosr)
    };
}

// Look direction from 3D vector
function lookDirection(dir) {
    const yaw = Math.atan2(dir.z, dir.x);
    const pitch = Math.atan2(dir.y, Math.hypot(dir.x, dir.z));
    return { yaw, pitch };
}
Slerp (Spherical Linear Interpolation)
Smoothly rotates 3D objects using quaternions, avoiding gimbal lock for camera/character rotation.
// Quaternion Slerp
function slerp(q1, q2, t) {
    let dot = q1.x*q2.x + q1.y*q2.y + q1.z*q2.z + q1.w*q2.w;

    // If negative, take the shorter path
    if (dot < 0) {
        q2 = { x: -q2.x, y: -q2.y, z: -q2.z, w: -q2.w };
        dot = -dot;
    }

    // If very close, use linear interpolation
    if (dot > 0.9995) {
        return {
            x: q1.x + t * (q2.x - q1.x),
            y: q1.y + t * (q2.y - q1.y),
            z: q1.z + t * (q2.z - q1.z),
            w: q1.w + t * (q2.w - q1.w)
        };
    }

    const theta = Math.acos(dot);
    const sinTheta = Math.sin(theta);
    const a = Math.sin((1 - t) * theta) / sinTheta;
    const b = Math.sin(t * theta) / sinTheta;

    return {
        x: a * q1.x + b * q2.x,
        y: a * q1.y + b * q2.y,
        z: a * q1.z + b * q2.z,
        w: a * q1.w + b * q2.w
    };
}

// Quaternion from axis-angle
function quatFromAxisAngle(axis, angle) {
    const halfAngle = angle / 2;
    const s = Math.sin(halfAngle);
    return {
        x: axis.x * s, y: axis.y * s, z: axis.z * s,
        w: Math.cos(halfAngle)
    };
}

// Quaternion multiplication
function quatMul(q1, q2) {
    return {
        w: q1.w*q2.w - q1.x*q2.x - q1.y*q2.y - q1.z*q2.z,
        x: q1.w*q2.x + q1.x*q2.w + q1.y*q2.z - q1.z*q2.y,
        y: q1.w*q2.y - q1.x*q2.z + q1.y*q2.w + q1.z*q2.x,
        z: q1.w*q2.z + q1.x*q2.y - q1.y*q2.x + q1.z*q2.w
    };
}

// Quaternion normalize
function quatNormalize(q) {
    const len = Math.sqrt(q.x*q.x + q.y*q.y + q.z*q.z + q.w*q.w);
    return { x: q.x/len, y: q.y/len, z: q.z/len, w: q.w/len };
}

// Quaternion to rotation matrix
function quatToMatrix(q) {
    const {x, y, z, w} = q;
    return [
        [1-2*(y*y+z*z), 2*(x*y-w*z), 2*(x*z+w*y)],
        [2*(x*y+w*z), 1-2*(x*x+z*z), 2*(y*z-w*x)],
        [2*(x*z-w*y), 2*(y*z+w*x), 1-2*(x*x+y*y)]
    ];
}

// Smooth camera rotation
class SmoothCamera {
    constructor() {
        this.currentQuat = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, w: 1 };
        this.targetQuat = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, w: 1 };
        this.smoothTime = 0.1;
    }
    setTarget(yaw, pitch) {
        const qy = quatFromAxisAngle({x:0,y:1,z:0}, yaw);
        const qx = quatFromAxisAngle({x:1,y:0,z:0}, pitch);
        this.targetQuat = quatMul(qy, qx);
    }
    update(dt) {
        const t = Math.min(1, dt / this.smoothTime);
        this.currentQuat = slerp(this.currentQuat, this.targetQuat, t);
        return this.currentQuat;
    }
}
Look-At Rotation Matrix
Generates a matrix forcing a 3D object to point its forward axis at a target coordinate.
// LookAt matrix (camera at eye, looking at target, up = Y)
function lookAtMatrix(eye, target, up = {x:0,y:1,z:0}) {
    const z = normalize({ x: eye.x-target.x, y: eye.y-target.y, z: eye.z-target.z });
    const x = normalize(cross(up, z));
    const y = cross(z, x);

    return [
        [x.x, x.y, x.z, -dot(x, eye)],
        [y.x, y.y, y.z, -dot(y, eye)],
        [z.x, z.y, z.z, -dot(z, eye)],
        [0, 0, 0, 1]
    ];
}

function normalize(v) {
    const len = Math.sqrt(v.x*v.x + v.y*v.y + v.z*v.z);
    return { x: v.x/len, y: v.y/len, z: v.z/len };
}

function cross(a, b) {
    return {
        x: a.y*b.z - a.z*b.y,
        y: a.z*b.x - a.x*b.z,
        z: a.x*b.y - a.y*b.x
    };
}

function dot(a, b) { return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z; }

// Look rotation quaternion
function lookRotation(forward, up = {x:0,y:1,z:0}) {
    const f = normalize(forward);
    const r = normalize(cross(up, f));
    const u = cross(f, r);

    // Quaternion from rotation matrix
    const m00 = r.x, m01 = r.y, m02 = r.z;
    const m10 = u.x, m11 = u.y, m12 = u.z;
    const m20 = f.x, m21 = f.y, m22 = f.z;

    const trace = m00 + m11 + m22;
    let w, x, y, z;

    if (trace > 0) {
        const s = 0.5 / Math.sqrt(trace + 1);
        w = 0.25 / s;
        x = (m21 - m12) * s;
        y = (m02 - m20) * s;
        z = (m10 - m01) * s;
    } else if (m00 > m11 && m00 > m22) {
        const s = 2 * Math.sqrt(1 + m00 - m11 - m22);
        w = (m21 - m12) / s;
        x = 0.25 * s;
        y = (m01 + m10) / s;
        z = (m02 + m20) / s;
    } else if (m11 > m22) {
        const s = 2 * Math.sqrt(1 + m11 - m00 - m22);
        w = (m02 - m20) / s;
        x = (m01 + m10) / s;
        y = 0.25 * s;
        z = (m12 + m21) / s;
    } else {
        const s = 2 * Math.sqrt(1 + m22 - m00 - m11);
        w = (m10 - m01) / s;
        x = (m02 + m20) / s;
        y = (m12 + m21) / s;
        z = 0.25 * s;
    }

    return quatNormalize({ x, y, z, w });
}

// Turret look-at
class Turret {
    constructor(pos) { this.pos = pos; this.angle = 0; this.pitch = 0; }
    lookAt(target) {
        const dx = target.x - this.pos.x;
        const dy = target.y - this.pos.y;
        const dz = target.z - this.pos.z;
        this.angle = Math.atan2(dz, dx);
        this.pitch = Math.atan2(dy, Math.hypot(dx, dz));
    }
}
Wave-Based Oscillation
Uses sine/cosine waves to create organic, looping patterns like floating loot or bobbing cameras.
// Basic sine wave
function sineWave(time, speed, amplitude, offset = 0) {
    return Math.sin(time * speed) * amplitude + offset;
}

// Cosine wave (phase shifted)
function cosineWave(time, speed, amplitude, offset = 0) {
    return Math.cos(time * speed) * amplitude + offset;
}

// Smooth oscillation between min/max
function oscillate(time, min, max, speed) {
    const mid = (min + max) / 2;
    const amp = (max - min) / 2;
    return mid + Math.sin(time * speed) * amp;
}

// Ping-pong (triangle wave)
function pingPong(time, min, max, speed) {
    const t = (time * speed) % 1;
    return min + (t < 0.5 ? t : 1 - t) * (max - min);
}

// Multiple harmonics (rich motion)
function multiWave(time, harmonics = 3, amplitude = 1) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i <= harmonics; i++) {
        sum += Math.sin(time * i) * amplitude / i;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Damped oscillation (settling)
function dampedOscillation(time, amplitude, frequency, damping) {
    return amplitude * Math.exp(-damping * time) * Math.sin(frequency * time);
}

// Bounce easing
function bounceEase(t) {
    if (t < 1/2.75) return 7.5625 * t * t;
    else if (t < 2/2.75) { t -= 1.5/2.75; return 7.5625*t*t+0.75; }
    else if (t < 2.5/2.75) { t -= 2.25/2.75; return 7.5625*t*t+0.9375; }
    else { t -= 2.625/2.75; return 7.5625*t*t+0.984375; }
}

// Floating item animation
class FloatingItem {
    constructor(x, y) {
        this.x = x; this.y = y;
        this.baseY = y;
        this.amplitude = 10;
        this.speed = 2;
        this.time = 0;
    }
    update(dt) {
        this.time += dt;
        this.y = this.baseY + sineWave(this.time, this.speed, this.amplitude);
    }
}

// Bobbing camera
class BobCamera {
    constructor() {
        this.offset = { x: 0, y: 0 };
        this.bobSpeed = 8;
        this.bobAmount = 0.05;
    }
    update(time, isMoving) {
        if (isMoving) {
            this.offset.x = Math.sin(time * this.bobSpeed) * this.bobAmount;
            this.offset.y = Math.cos(time * this.bobSpeed * 2) * this.bobAmount * 0.5;
        } else {
            this.offset.x *= 0.9;
            this.offset.y *= 0.9;
        }
    }
}

// Flickering light
class FlickerLight {
    constructor(baseIntensity = 1) {
        this.base = baseIntensity;
        this.time = 0;
    }
    update(dt) {
        this.time += dt;
        const flicker = sineWave(this.time, 15, 0.1) + sineWave(this.time, 23, 0.05);
        return this.base * (1 + flicker);
    }
}
Flood Fill
Finds connected areas on a grid for matching blocks, fog of war, explosions, or painting tiles.
// 4-directional flood fill (iterative)
function floodFill4(grid, sx, sy, targetColor, fillColor) {
    if (grid[sy][sx] === fillColor) return;
    const stack = [[sx, sy]];
    const rows = grid.length, cols = grid[0].length;

    while (stack.length > 0) {
        const [x, y] = stack.pop();
        if (x < 0 || x >= cols || y < 0 || y >= rows) continue;
        if (grid[y][x] !== targetColor) continue;

        grid[y][x] = fillColor;
        stack.push([x+1,y],[x-1,y],[x,y+1],[x,y-1]);
    }
    return grid;
}

// 8-directional flood fill
function floodFill8(grid, sx, sy, targetColor, fillColor) {
    const stack = [[sx, sy]];
    const rows = grid.length, cols = grid[0].length;

    while (stack.length > 0) {
        const [x, y] = stack.pop();
        if (x < 0 || x >= cols || y < 0 || y >= rows) continue;
        if (grid[y][x] !== targetColor) continue;

        grid[y][x] = fillColor;
        for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
            for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
                if (dx !== 0 || dy !== 0) stack.push([x+dx, y+dy]);
            }
        }
    }
    return grid;
}

// BFS flood fill (returns visited cells)
function floodFillBFS(grid, sx, sy, targetColor, fillColor) {
    const queue = [[sx, sy]];
    const visited = new Set();
    const cells = [];
    const rows = grid.length, cols = grid[0].length;

    while (queue.length > 0) {
        const [x, y] = queue.shift();
        const key = `${x},${y}`;
        if (visited.has(key)) continue;
        if (x < 0 || x >= cols || y < 0 || y >= rows) continue;
        if (grid[y][x] !== targetColor) continue;

        visited.add(key);
        grid[y][x] = fillColor;
        cells.push({x, y});

        queue.push([x+1,y],[x-1,y],[x,y+1],[x,y-1]);
    }
    return cells;
}

// Count connected regions
function countRegions(grid) {
    let count = 0;
    for (let y = 0; y < grid.length; y++) {
        for (let x = 0; x < grid[0].length; x++) {
            if (grid[y][x] === 0) {
                floodFill4(grid, x, y, 0, ++count + 1);
            }
        }
    }
    return count;
}

// Candy Crush match detection
function findMatches(grid, minMatch = 3) {
    const matches = [];
    const rows = grid.length, cols = grid[0].length;

    // Horizontal
    for (let y = 0; y < rows; y++) {
        let count = 1;
        for (let x = 1; x <= cols; x++) {
            if (x < cols && grid[y][x] === grid[y][x-1]) count++;
            else {
                if (count >= minMatch) {
                    matches.push({ type: 'h', y, x: x-count, length: count });
                }
                count = 1;
            }
        }
    }

    // Vertical
    for (let x = 0; x < cols; x++) {
        let count = 1;
        for (let y = 1; y <= rows; y++) {
            if (y < rows && grid[y][x] === grid[y-1][x]) count++;
            else {
                if (count >= minMatch) {
                    matches.push({ type: 'v', x, y: y-count, length: count });
                }
                count = 1;
            }
        }
    }

    return matches;
}

// Fog of war reveal
function revealFog(fogGrid, cx, cy, radius) {
    const cells = [];
    for (let y = cy - radius; y <= cy + radius; y++) {
        for (let x = cx - radius; x <= cx + radius; x++) {
            if (Math.hypot(x-cx, y-cy) <= radius && fogGrid[y] && fogGrid[y][x] !== undefined) {
                fogGrid[y][x] = 1;
                cells.push({x, y});
            }
        }
    }
    return cells;
}
Multi-Dimensional Coordinate Mapping
Transforms 1D index to 2D grid coordinates using modulo and division for inventories and tilemaps.
// 1D to 2D
function indexTo2D(index, width) {
    return {
        x: index % width,
        y: Math.floor(index / width)
    };
}

// 2D to 1D
function coordsToIndex(x, y, width) {
    return y * width + x;
}

// 1D to 3D
function indexTo3D(index, width, height) {
    return {
        x: index % width,
        y: Math.floor(index / width) % height,
        z: Math.floor(index / (width * height))
    };
}

// 3D to 1D
function coordsToIndex3D(x, y, z, width, height) {
    return z * width * height + y * width + x;
}

// Flat array to 2D grid
function arrayToGrid(arr, width) {
    const grid = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += width) {
        grid.push(arr.slice(i, i + width));
    }
    return grid;
}

// 2D grid to flat array
function gridToArray(grid) {
    return grid.flat();
}

// Inventory slot mapping
class Inventory {
    constructor(cols, rows) {
        this.cols = cols;
        this.rows = rows;
        this.slots = new Array(cols * rows).fill(null);
    }

    getItem(col, row) {
        return this.slots[row * this.cols + col];
    }

    setItem(col, row, item) {
        this.slots[row * this.cols + col] = item;
    }

    getSlot(index) {
        return indexTo2D(index, this.cols);
    }

    getIndex(col, row) {
        return coordsToIndex(col, row, this.cols);
    }

    // Find empty slot
    findEmpty() {
        const idx = this.slots.indexOf(null);
        return idx >= 0 ? indexTo2D(idx, this.cols) : null;
    }

    // Swap items
    swap(col1, row1, col2, row2) {
        const i1 = this.getIndex(col1, row1);
        const i2 = this.getIndex(col2, row2);
        [this.slots[i1], this.slots[i2]] = [this.slots[i2], this.slots[i1]];
    }
}

// Tilemap coordinate helpers
class TileMap {
    constructor(width, height, tileSize) {
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
        this.tileSize = tileSize;
    }

    worldToTile(wx, wy) {
        return {
            x: Math.floor(wx / this.tileSize),
            y: Math.floor(wy / this.tileSize)
        };
    }

    tileToWorld(tx, ty) {
        return {
            x: tx * this.tileSize + this.tileSize / 2,
            y: ty * this.tileSize + this.tileSize / 2
        };
    }

    isValid(tx, ty) {
        return tx >= 0 && tx < this.width && ty >= 0 && ty < this.height;
    }

    getNeighbors(tx, ty) {
        const dirs = [[0,1],[0,-1],[1,0],[-1,0]];
        return dirs
            .map(([dx,dy]) => ({x: tx+dx, y: ty+dy}))
            .filter(p => this.isValid(p.x, p.y));
    }
}

// Spiral coordinate mapping
function spiralCoords(radius) {
    const coords = [];
    let x = 0, y = 0, dx = 0, dy = -1;
    for (let i = 0; i < (2*radius+1)*(2*radius+1); i++) {
        if (Math.abs(x) <= radius && Math.abs(y) <= radius) coords.push({x, y});
        if (x === y || (x < 0 && x === -y) || (x > 0 && x === 1-y)) {
            [dx, dy] = [-dy, dx];
        }
        x += dx; y += dy;
    }
    return coords;
}
📐 VECTOR PROJECTION & ANGLE RELATIONSHIP ALGORITHMS
Algorithms using dot and cross products to analyze how vectors relate to one another in space
Dot Product for Field of View
Calculates cosine of angle between vectors to check if player is inside enemy's vision cone.
// Check if target is in FOV
function isInFOV(forward, toTarget, fovAngle) {
    const dot = normalize(forward).x * normalize(toTarget).x +
                normalize(forward).y * normalize(toTarget).y;
    return dot >= Math.cos(fovAngle / 2);
}

// Dot product = |a||b|cos(theta)
function dotProduct(a, b) {
    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y;
}

// 3D dot product
function dot3D(a, b) {
    return a.x * b.x + a.y * b.y + a.z * b.z;
}

// Angle between vectors (in radians)
function angleBetween(a, b) {
    const dot = dotProduct(normalize(a), normalize(b));
    return Math.acos(Math.min(1, Math.max(-1, dot)));
}

// Check backface (is polygon facing away?)
function isBackface(normal, viewDir) {
    return dot3D(normal, viewDir) < 0;
}

// Smooth FOV falloff
function fovStrength(forward, toTarget, fovAngle, falloff = 0.1) {
    const dot = normalize(forward).x * normalize(toTarget).x +
                normalize(forward).y * normalize(toTarget).y;
    const cosHalfFov = Math.cos(fovAngle / 2);
    if (dot >= cosHalfFov) return 1;
    return Math.max(0, 1 - (cosHalfFov - dot) / falloff);
}

// Vision cone check with distance
function canSeeTarget(eye, forward, target, maxDist, fovAngle) {
    const toTarget = { x: target.x - eye.x, y: target.y - eye.y };
    const dist = Math.hypot(toTarget.x, toTarget.y);
    if (dist > maxDist) return false;
    return isInFOV(forward, toTarget, fovAngle);
}

// Light intensity based on surface angle
function lightIntensity(lightDir, surfaceNormal) {
    return Math.max(0, dotProduct(normalize(lightDir), normalize(surfaceNormal)));
}

// Lambertian reflection
function lambert(normal, lightDir) {
    return Math.max(0, dot3D(normalize(normal), normalize(lightDir)));
}
Cross Product for Surface Normals
Multiplies two 3D vectors to find a third vector perfectly perpendicular to both.
// Cross product (A x B)
function crossProduct(a, b) {
    return {
        x: a.y * b.z - a.z * b.y,
        y: a.z * b.x - a.x * b.z,
        z: a.x * b.y - a.y * b.x
    };
}

// Surface normal from triangle
function triangleNormal(v0, v1, v2) {
    const edge1 = { x: v1.x-v0.x, y: v1.y-v0.y, z: v1.z-v0.z };
    const edge2 = { x: v2.x-v0.x, y: v2.y-v0.y, z: v2.z-v0.z };
    return normalize(crossProduct(edge1, edge2));
}

// Face normal from quad (2 triangles)
function quadNormal(v0, v1, v2, v3) {
    const n1 = triangleNormal(v0, v1, v2);
    const n2 = triangleNormal(v0, v2, v3);
    return normalize({
        x: (n1.x + n2.x) / 2,
        y: (n1.y + n2.y) / 2,
        z: (n1.z + n2.z) / 2
    });
}

// Terrain slope normal
function terrainNormal(heightMap, x, y) {
    const hL = heightMap[y][x-1] || heightMap[y][x];
    const hR = heightMap[y][x+1] || heightMap[y][x];
    const hU = heightMap[y-1] ? heightMap[y-1][x] : heightMap[y][x];
    const hD = heightMap[y+1] ? heightMap[y+1][x] : heightMap[y][x];
    return normalize({ x: hL - hR, y: 2, z: hU - hD });
}

// Winding order check (CW vs CCW)
function windingOrder(v0, v1, v2) {
    const cross = (v1.x-v0.x)*(v2.y-v0.y) - (v1.y-v0.y)*(v2.x-v0.x);
    return cross > 0 ? 'CCW' : 'CW';
}

// Reflect vector off normal
function reflect(incident, normal) {
    const dot = 2 * dotProduct(incident, normal);
    return {
        x: incident.x - dot * normal.x,
        y: incident.y - dot * normal.y,
        z: incident.z - dot * normal.z
    };
}

// Normalize vector
function normalize(v) {
    const len = Math.sqrt(v.x*v.x + v.y*v.y + (v.z||0)*(v.z||0));
    return { x: v.x/len, y: v.y/len, z: (v.z||0)/len };
}

// Calculate tangent and bitangent for normal mapping
function tangentBitangent(v0, v1, v2, uv0, uv1, uv2) {
    const edge1 = { x: v1.x-v0.x, y: v1.y-v0.y, z: v1.z-v0.z };
    const edge2 = { x: v2.x-v0.x, y: v2.y-v0.y, z: v2.z-v0.z };
    const deltaUV1 = { x: uv1.x-uv0.x, y: uv1.y-uv0.y };
    const deltaUV2 = { x: uv2.x-uv0.x, y: uv2.y-uv0.y };

    const f = 1.0 / (deltaUV1.x * deltaUV2.y - deltaUV2.x * deltaUV1.y);

    const tangent = {
        x: f * (deltaUV2.y * edge1.x - deltaUV1.y * edge2.x),
        y: f * (deltaUV2.y * edge1.y - deltaUV1.y * edge2.y),
        z: f * (deltaUV2.y * edge1.z - deltaUV1.y * edge2.z)
    };
    const bitangent = {
        x: f * (-deltaUV2.x * edge1.x + deltaUV1.x * edge2.x),
        y: f * (-deltaUV2.x * edge1.y + deltaUV1.x * edge2.y),
        z: f * (-deltaUV2.x * edge1.z + deltaUV1.x * edge2.z)
    };
    return { tangent, bitangent };
}
Vector Projection (Wall Sliding)
Projects one vector onto another for wall-sliding collision response instead of stopping.
// Project A onto B
function project(a, b) {
    const dotAB = dotProduct(a, b);
    const dotBB = dotProduct(b, b);
    return {
        x: (dotAB / dotBB) * b.x,
        y: (dotAB / dotBB) * b.y
    };
}

// 3D projection
function project3D(a, b) {
    const dotAB = dotProduct(a, b);
    const dotBB = dotProduct(a, b);
    const scalar = dotAB / dotBB;
    return { x: scalar*b.x, y: scalar*b.y, z: scalar*b.z };
}

// Reject (perpendicular component)
function reject(a, b) {
    const proj = project(a, b);
    return { x: a.x - proj.x, y: a.y - proj.y };
}

// Wall sliding collision
function wallSlide(velocity, wallNormal) {
    const proj = project(velocity, wallNormal);
    return {
        x: velocity.x - proj.x,
        y: velocity.y - proj.y
    };
}

// Sliding along wall with friction
function wallSlideFriction(velocity, wallNormal, friction = 0.8) {
    const slide = wallSlide(velocity, wallNormal);
    return {
        x: slide.x * friction,
        y: slide.y * friction
    };
}

// Ramp sliding (3D)
function rampSlide(velocity, rampNormal) {
    const proj = project3D(velocity, rampNormal);
    return {
        x: velocity.x - proj.x,
        y: velocity.y - proj.y,
        z: velocity.z - proj.z
    };
}

// Reflect off surface
function reflectVelocity(velocity, normal, restitution = 1) {
    const dotVN = dotProduct(velocity, normal);
    return {
        x: velocity.x - (1 + restitution) * dotVN * normal.x,
        y: velocity.y - (1 + restitution) * dotVN * normal.y
    };
}

// Bounce off wall
function bounce(velocity, wallNormal, bounciness = 0.7) {
    const normal = normalize(wallNormal);
    const dotVN = dotProduct(velocity, normal);
    return {
        x: (velocity.x - 2 * dotVN * normal.x) * bounciness,
        y: (velocity.y - 2 * dotVN * normal.y) * bounciness
    };
}

// Component along direction
function componentAlong(velocity, direction) {
    return dotProduct(velocity, normalize(direction));
}

// Split velocity into normal and tangent
function decomposeVelocity(velocity, normal) {
    const normalComp = project(velocity, normal);
    const tangentComp = { x: velocity.x - normalComp.x, y: velocity.y - normalComp.y };
    return { normal: normalComp, tangent: tangentComp };
}
Distance to Line Segment
Finds closest point on a line segment to an external point for laser damage or rail-shooter tracking.
// Closest point on line segment to point
function closestPointOnSegment(p, a, b) {
    const ab = { x: b.x - a.x, y: b.y - a.y };
    const ap = { x: p.x - a.x, y: p.y - a.y };
    const t = dotProduct(ap, ab) / dotProduct(ab, ab);
    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));

    return {
        x: a.x + clamped * ab.x,
        y: a.y + clamped * ab.y,
        t: clamped
    };
}

// Distance from point to line segment
function distToSegment(p, a, b) {
    const closest = closestPointOnSegment(p, a, b);
    return Math.hypot(p.x - closest.x, p.y - closest.y);
}

// 3D version
function closestPointOnSegment3D(p, a, b) {
    const ab = { x: b.x-a.x, y: b.y-a.y, z: b.z-a.z };
    const ap = { x: p.x-a.x, y: p.y-a.y, z: p.z-a.z };
    const t = dotProduct(ap, ab) / dotProduct(ab, ab);
    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
    return {
        x: a.x + clamped*ab.x,
        y: a.y + clamped*ab.y,
        z: a.z + clamped*ab.z,
        t: clamped
    };
}

// Point to infinite line distance
function distToLine(p, a, b) {
    const ab = { x: b.x-a.x, y: b.y-a.y };
    const ap = { x: p.x-a.x, y: p.y-a.y };
    return Math.abs(ab.x*ap.y - ab.y*ap.x) / Math.hypot(ab.x, ab.y);
}

// Segment to segment closest points
function closestPointsBetweenSegments(a1, b1, a2, b2) {
    const d1 = { x: b1.x-a1.x, y: b1.y-a1.y };
    const d2 = { x: b2.x-a2.x, y: b2.y-a2.y };
    const r = { x: a1.x-a2.x, y: a1.y-a2.y };

    const a = dotProduct(d1, d1);
    const e = dotProduct(d2, d2);
    const f = dotProduct(d2, r);

    let s, t;

    if (a <= 1e-6 && e <= 1e-6) {
        s = t = 0;
    } else if (a <= 1e-6) {
        s = 0; t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, f / e));
    } else {
        const c = dotProduct(d1, r);
        if (e <= 1e-6) {
            t = 0; s = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, -c / a));
        } else {
            const b = dotProduct(d1, d2);
            const denom = a*e - b*b;
            s = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (b*f - c*e) / denom));
            t = (b*s + f) / e;
            if (t < 0) { t = 0; s = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, -c / a)); }
            else if (t > 1) { t = 1; s = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (b - c) / a)); }
        }
    }

    return {
        p1: { x: a1.x + s*d1.x, y: a1.y + s*d1.y },
        p2: { x: a2.x + t*d2.x, y: a2.y + t*d2.y },
        s, t
    };
}

// Laser hit detection
function laserHitsSegment(laserOrigin, laserDir, segmentA, segmentB) {
    const closest = closestPointOnSegment(laserOrigin, segmentA, segmentB);
    const toClosest = { x: closest.x - laserOrigin.x, y: closest.y - laserOrigin.y };
    const dot = dotProduct(normalize(laserDir), normalize(toClosest));
    return dot > 0.99; // Nearly parallel = hit
}
🔵 BASIC SHAPES INTERSECTION ALGORITHMS
Analytical geometry - fastest algorithms physics engines use to check bounding shape overlap
AABB vs. AABB Collision
Checks if two unrotated rectangles/boxes overlap using simple min/max coordinate inequalities.
// 2D AABB overlap test
function aabbOverlap(a, b) {
    return a.minX < b.maxX && a.maxX > b.minX &&
           a.minY < b.maxY && a.maxY > b.minY;
}

// 3D AABB overlap test
function aabbOverlap3D(a, b) {
    return a.minX < b.maxX && a.maxX > b.minX &&
           a.minY < b.maxY && a.maxY > b.minY &&
           a.minZ < b.maxZ && a.maxZ > b.minZ;
}

// AABB class
class AABB {
    constructor(minX, minY, maxX, maxY) {
        this.minX = minX; this.minY = minY;
        this.maxX = maxX; this.maxY = maxY;
    }
    get width() { return this.maxX - this.minX; }
    get height() { return this.maxY - this.minY; }
    get centerX() { return (this.minX + this.maxX) / 2; }
    get centerY() { return (this.minY + this.maxY) / 2; }

    overlaps(other) {
        return aabbOverlap(this, other);
    }

    containsPoint(x, y) {
        return x >= this.minX && x <= this.maxX &&
               y >= this.minY && y <= this.maxY;
    }

    // Get overlap area
    overlapArea(other) {
        const overlapX = Math.min(this.maxX, other.maxX) - Math.max(this.minX, other.minX);
        const overlapY = Math.min(this.maxY, other.maxY) - Math.max(this.minY, other.minY);
        if (overlapX <= 0 || overlapY <= 0) return 0;
        return overlapX * overlapY;
    }

    // Merge two AABBs
    merge(other) {
        return new AABB(
            Math.min(this.minX, other.minX),
            Math.min(this.minY, other.minY),
            Math.max(this.maxX, other.maxX),
            Math.max(this.maxY, other.maxY)
        );
    }

    // Expand by margin
    expand(margin) {
        return new AABB(
            this.minX - margin, this.minY - margin,
            this.maxX + margin, this.maxY + margin
        );
    }
}

// Create AABB from center and size
function aabbFromCenter(cx, cy, halfW, halfH) {
    return new AABB(cx - halfW, cy - halfH, cx + halfW, cy + halfH);
}

// Sweep AABB movement
function sweptAABB(a, b, velX, velY) {
    let tEntry = 0, tExit = 1;
    // X axis
    if (velX !== 0) {
        const t1 = (b.minX - a.maxX) / velX;
        const t2 = (b.maxX - a.minX) / velX;
        tEntry = Math.max(tEntry, Math.min(t1, t2));
        tExit = Math.min(tExit, Math.max(t1, t2));
    } else if (a.maxX <= b.minX || a.minX >= b.maxX) return null;
    // Y axis
    if (velY !== 0) {
        const t1 = (b.minY - a.maxY) / velY;
        const t2 = (b.maxY - a.minY) / velY;
        tEntry = Math.max(tEntry, Math.min(t1, t2));
        tExit = Math.min(tExit, Math.max(t1, t2));
    } else if (a.maxY <= b.minY || a.minY >= b.maxY) return null;

    if (tEntry <= tExit && tExit > 0 && tEntry < 1) {
        return { time: tEntry, normal: { x: velX > 0 ? -1 : 1, y: 0 } };
    }
    return null;
}
Circle/Sphere vs. Circle/Sphere
Uses Pythagorean theorem to check distance between centers against sum of radii.
// 2D circle overlap
function circleOverlap(x1, y1, r1, x2, y2, r2) {
    const dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1;
    const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    const radiiSum = r1 + r2;
    return distSq <= radiiSum * radiiSum;
}

// Returns collision info
function circleCollision(x1, y1, r1, x2, y2, r2) {
    const dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    const radiiSum = r1 + r2;

    if (dist >= radiiSum) return null;

    const nx = dx / dist, ny = dy / dist;
    const overlap = radiiSum - dist;

    return {
        point: { x: x1 + nx * r1, y: y1 + ny * r1 },
        normal: { x: nx, y: ny },
        depth: overlap
    };
}

// 3D sphere overlap
function sphereOverlap(s1, s2) {
    const dx = s2.x - s1.x, dy = s2.y - s1.y, dz = s2.z - s1.z;
    const distSq = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz;
    const radiiSum = s1.radius + s2.radius;
    return distSq <= radiiSum * radiiSum;
}

// Sphere collision response
function sphereResponse(s1, s2) {
    const dx = s2.x - s1.x, dy = s2.y - s1.y, dz = s2.z - s1.z;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);
    if (dist === 0) return null;

    const nx = dx/dist, ny = dy/dist, nz = dz/dist;
    const overlap = (s1.radius + s2.radius) - dist;

    // Move spheres apart equally
    return {
        s1: { x: s1.x - nx*overlap/2, y: s1.y - ny*overlap/2, z: s1.z - nz*overlap/2 },
        s2: { x: s2.x + nx*overlap/2, y: s2.y + ny*overlap/2, z: s2.z + nz*overlap/2 }
    };
}

// Point inside circle
function pointInCircle(px, py, cx, cy, r) {
    return (px-cx)*(px-cx) + (py-cy)*(py-cy) <= r*r;
}

// Circle-AABB closest point
function circleAABB(x, y, r, box) {
    const closestX = Math.max(box.minX, Math.min(x, box.maxX));
    const closestY = Math.max(box.minY, Math.min(y, box.maxY));
    const dx = x - closestX, dy = y - closestY;
    return dx*dx + dy*dy <= r*r;
}

// Sphere cast (moving sphere)
function sphereCast(origin, target, radius, obstacle) {
    const dx = target.x - origin.x;
    const dy = target.y - origin.y;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (dist === 0) return null;

    // Expand obstacle by radius
    const expanded = {
        minX: obstacle.minX - radius,
        maxX: obstacle.maxX + radius,
        minY: obstacle.minY - radius,
        maxY: obstacle.maxY + radius
    };

    // Ray vs expanded AABB
    const t = rayAABB(origin.x, origin.y, dx/dist, dy/dist, expanded);
    if (t !== null && t <= dist) {
        return t;
    }
    return null;
}
AABB vs. Circle Intersection
Finds closest point on AABB to circle center for platformer physics.
// AABB vs Circle
function aabbCircleOverlap(aabb, cx, cy, r) {
    const closestX = Math.max(aabb.minX, Math.min(cx, aabb.maxX));
    const closestY = Math.max(aabb.minY, Math.min(cy, aabb.maxY));
    const dx = cx - closestX, dy = cy - closestY;
    return dx*dx + dy*dy <= r*r;
}

// Returns collision info
function aabbCircleCollision(aabb, cx, cy, r) {
    const closestX = Math.max(aabb.minX, Math.min(cx, aabb.maxX));
    const closestY = Math.max(aabb.minY, Math.min(cy, aabb.maxY));
    const dx = cx - closestX, dy = cy - closestY;
    const distSq = dx*dx + dy*dy;

    if (distSq >= r*r) return null;

    const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);
    const nx = dist > 0 ? dx/dist : 0;
    const ny = dist > 0 ? dy/dist : -1;

    return {
        point: { x: closestX, y: closestY },
        normal: { x: nx, y: ny },
        depth: r - dist
    };
}

// Platformer: circle on AABB
function onPlatform(circleX, circleY, circleR, aabb, maxSlope = 0.5) {
    const closestX = Math.max(aabb.minX, Math.min(circleX, aabb.maxX));
    const closestY = Math.max(aabb.minY, Math.min(circleY, aabb.maxY));
    const dx = circleX - closestX, dy = circleY - closestY;
    const distSq = dx*dx + dy*dy;

    if (distSq > circleR*circleR) return null;

    const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);
    if (dist === 0) return { nx: 0, ny: -1, ground: true };

    const nx = dx/dist, ny = dy/dist;
    if (ny < -maxSlope) return null; // Too steep = wall

    return { nx, ny, ground: ny < -0.7, depth: circleR - dist };
}

// AABB from position and size
function aabbFromPos(x, y, w, h) {
    return { minX: x-w/2, minY: y-h/2, maxX: x+w/2, maxY: y+h/2 };
}

// Sphere vs AABB (3D)
function sphereAABB3D(sphere, box) {
    let distSq = 0;
    for (const axis of ['x','y','z']) {
        const min = box[`min${axis.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+axis.slice(1)}`];
        const max = box[`max${axis.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+axis.slice(1)}`];
        const v = sphere[axis];
        if (v < min) distSq += (min-v)*(min-v);
        else if (v > max) distSq += (v-max)*(v-max);
    }
    return distSq <= sphere.radius*sphere.radius;
}
Ray vs. Plane Intersection
Calculates exact point where an infinite line passes through a flat 3D surface.
// Ray vs infinite plane
function rayPlaneIntersect(rayOrigin, rayDir, planeNormal, planeDist) {
    const denom = dotProduct(rayDir, planeNormal);
    if (Math.abs(denom) < 1e-6) return null; // Parallel

    const t = (planeDist - dotProduct(rayOrigin, planeNormal)) / denom;
    if (t < 0) return null; // Behind ray

    return {
        t,
        point: {
            x: rayOrigin.x + rayDir.x * t,
            y: rayOrigin.y + rayDir.y * t,
            z: rayOrigin.z + rayDir.z * t
        }
    };
}

// Ray vs plane defined by 3 points
function rayPlaneFromPoints(rayOrigin, rayDir, p1, p2, p3) {
    const v1 = { x: p2.x-p1.x, y: p2.y-p1.y, z: p2.z-p1.z };
    const v2 = { x: p3.x-p1.x, y: p3.y-p1.y, z: p3.z-p1.z };
    const normal = normalize(crossProduct(v1, v2));
    const dist = dotProduct(normal, p1);
    return rayPlaneIntersect(rayOrigin, rayDir, normal, dist);
}

// Ray vs triangle (Moller-Trumbore)
function rayTriangleIntersect(rayO, rayD, v0, v1, v2) {
    const e1 = { x:v1.x-v0.x, y:v1.y-v0.y, z:v1.z-v0.z };
    const e2 = { x:v2.x-v0.x, y:v2.y-v0.y, z:v2.z-v0.z };
    const h = crossProduct(rayD, e2);
    const a = dotProduct(e1, h);

    if (a > -1e-6 && a < 1e-6) return null;

    const f = 1/a;
    const s = { x:rayO.x-v0.x, y:rayO.y-v0.y, z:rayO.z-v0.z };
    const u = f * dotProduct(s, h);

    if (u < 0 || u > 1) return null;

    const q = crossProduct(s, e1);
    const v = f * dotProduct(rayD, q);

    if (v < 0 || u+v > 1) return null;

    const t = f * dotProduct(e2, q);
    if (t > 1e-6) {
        return {
            t,
            point: { x:rayO.x+rayD.x*t, y:rayO.y+rayD.y*t, z:rayO.z+rayD.z*t },
            barycentric: { u, v, w: 1-u-v }
        };
    }
    return null;
}

// Ray vs horizontal plane (Y=0)
function rayGroundIntersect(rayO, rayD) {
    if (Math.abs(rayD.y) < 1e-6) return null;
    const t = -rayO.y / rayD.y;
    if (t < 0) return null;
    return {
        t,
        point: { x:rayO.x+rayD.x*t, y:0, z:rayO.z+rayD.z*t }
    };
}

// Mouse ray from screen to world (simplified)
function screenToWorldRay(mx, my, viewW, viewH, camPos) {
    const ndcX = (mx / viewW) * 2 - 1;
    const ndcY = 1 - (my / viewH) * 2;
    const dir = normalize({ x: ndcX, y: ndcY, z: -1 });
    return { origin: camPos, dir };
}

// Ray-AABB intersection
function rayAABB(rayOx, rayOy, dirX, dirY, box) {
    let tmin = -Infinity, tmax = Infinity;
    const invDirX = 1/dirX, invDirY = 1/dirY;

    let t1 = (box.minX - rayOx) * invDirX;
    let t2 = (box.maxX - rayOx) * invDirX;
    tmin = Math.max(tmin, Math.min(t1, t2));
    tmax = Math.min(tmax, Math.max(t1, t2));

    t1 = (box.minY - rayOy) * invDirY;
    t2 = (box.maxY - rayOy) * invDirY;
    tmin = Math.max(tmin, Math.min(t1, t2));
    tmax = Math.min(tmax, Math.max(t1, t2));

    return tmin <= tmax && tmax >= 0 ? tmin : null;
}

// Dot/cross helpers
function dotProduct(a, b) { return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + (a.z||0)*(b.z||0); }
function crossProduct(a, b) {
    return {
        x: a.y*(b.z||0) - (a.z||0)*b.y,
        y: (a.z||0)*b.x - a.x*(b.z||0),
        z: a.x*b.y - a.y*b.x
    };
}
function normalize(v) {
    const l = Math.sqrt(v.x*v.x + v.y*v.y + (v.z||0)*(v.z||0));
    return { x:v.x/l, y:v.y/l, z:(v.z||0)/l };
}
🔷 ADVANCED GEOMETRIC BOUNDARY ALGORITHMS
Complex structural geometry for advanced physics, vision, and lighting systems
Separating Axis Theorem (SAT)
Projects convex polygon vertices onto orthogonal axes to detect collision gaps.
// SAT for convex polygons
function satCollision(polyA, polyB) {
    const axes = [...getAxes(polyA), ...getAxes(polyB)];
    let minOverlap = Infinity, smallestAxis = null;

    for (const axis of axes) {
        const projA = project(polyA, axis);
        const projB = project(polyB, axis);
        const overlap = Math.min(projA.max, projB.max) - Math.max(projA.min, projB.min);

        if (overlap <= 0) return null; // Gap found = no collision

        if (overlap < minOverlap) {
            minOverlap = overlap;
            smallestAxis = axis;
        }
    }

    // Ensure normal points from A to B
    const centerA = centroid(polyA);
    const centerB = centroid(polyB);
    const d = { x: centerB.x - centerA.x, y: centerB.y - centerA.y };
    if (dotProduct(d, smallestAxis) < 0) {
        smallestAxis = { x: -smallestAxis.x, y: -smallestAxis.y };
    }

    return { overlap: minOverlap, normal: smallestAxis };
}

// Get potential separating axes (edge normals)
function getAxes(poly) {
    const axes = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < poly.length; i++) {
        const j = (i + 1) % poly.length;
        const edge = { x: poly[j].x - poly[i].x, y: poly[j].y - poly[i].y };
        axes.push({ x: -edge.y, y: edge.x }); // Perpendicular
    }
    return axes;
}

// Project polygon onto axis
function project(poly, axis) {
    let min = Infinity, max = -Infinity;
    for (const p of poly) {
        const proj = p.x * axis.x + p.y * axis.y;
        min = Math.min(min, proj);
        max = Math.max(max, proj);
    }
    return { min, max };
}

// Centroid of polygon
function centroid(poly) {
    let cx = 0, cy = 0;
    for (const p of poly) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
    return { x: cx / poly.length, y: cy / poly.length };
}

// SAT with contact point
function satWithContact(polyA, polyB) {
    const result = satCollision(polyA, polyB);
    if (!result) return null;

    // Find contact points
    const contacts = [];
    for (const v of polyB) {
        if (isInsideConvex(polyA, v)) contacts.push(v);
    }
    for (const v of polyA) {
        if (isInsideConvex(polyB, v)) contacts.push(v);
    }

    return { ...result, contacts };
}

// Point inside convex polygon
function isInsideConvex(poly, point) {
