Simulation of physical systems, contact detection, and mesh destruction
SPH Fluid Simulation (Spatial Hash Grid)
Breaks space into grid indices to calculate interactions, pressures, and movement for thousands of water particles.
// SPH Fluid Simulation with Spatial Hashing
class SPHFluid {
    constructor(cellSize, smoothingRadius) {
        this.cellSize = cellSize;
        this.h = smoothingRadius;
        this.particles = [];
        this.grid = new Map();
    }

    hash(x, y) {
        const cx = Math.floor(x / this.cellSize);
        const cy = Math.floor(y / this.cellSize);
        return `${cx},${cy}`;
    }

    insert(particle) {
        const k = this.hash(particle.x, particle.y);
        if (!this.grid.has(k)) this.grid.set(k, []);
        this.grid.get(k).push(particle);
    }

    query(x, y, radius) {
        const results = [];
        const cx = Math.floor(x / this.cellSize);
        const cy = Math.floor(y / this.cellSize);
        const r = Math.ceil(radius / this.cellSize);
        for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
            for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
                const k = `${cx+dx},${cy+dy}`;
                if (this.grid.has(k)) results.push(...this.grid.get(k));
            }
        }
        return results;
    }

    kernel(r) {
        const q = 1 - r / this.h;
        return q >= 0 ? (15 / (Math.PI * this.h * this.h * this.h)) * q * q * q : 0;
    }

    density(p, neighbors) {
        let rho = 0;
        for (const n of neighbors) {
            const d = Math.sqrt((p.x-n.x)**2 + (p.y-n.y)**2);
            if (d < this.h) rho += this.kernel(d);
        }
        return rho;
    }

    pressure(p, neighbors) {
        const rho = this.density(p, neighbors);
        return 2000 * (rho - 1000); // rest density = 1000
    }

    simulate(dt) {
        this.grid.clear();
        this.particles.forEach(p => this.insert(p));

        for (const p of this.particles) {
            const neighbors = this.query(p.x, p.y, this.h);
            const pres = this.pressure(p, neighbors);

            // Pressure force
            let fx = 0, fy = 0;
            for (const n of neighbors) {
                if (n === p) continue;
                const d = Math.sqrt((p.x-n.x)**2 + (p.y-n.y)**2);
                if (d > 0 && d < this.h) {
                    const sharedP = (pres + this.pressure(n, neighbors)) / 2;
                    const grad = (15 / (Math.PI * this.h**4)) * (1 - d/this.h)**2;
                    fx -= sharedP * (p.x - n.x) / d * grad / this.density(n, neighbors);
                    fy -= sharedP * (p.y - n.y) / d * grad / this.density(n, neighbors);
                }
            }

            // Gravity + viscosity + pressure
            p.vx += (fx / this.density(p, neighbors)) * dt;
            p.vy += (fy / this.density(p, neighbors) + 9.81) * dt;
            p.x += p.vx * dt;
            p.y += p.vy * dt;
        }
    }
}
Fracture Voronoi Decomposition
Cuts a 3D model into smaller, clean physics pieces based on impact locations for realistic mesh shattering.
// Fracture Voronoi Decomposition
class FractureVoronoi {
    constructor(vertices, faces) {
        this.vertices = vertices;
        this.faces = faces;
    }

    generateFracturePoints(center, numPoints, radius) {
        const points = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < numPoints; i++) {
            const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);
            const r = radius * Math.cbrt(Math.random());
            points.push({
                x: center.x + r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta),
                y: center.y + r * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta),
                z: center.z + r * Math.cos(phi)
            });
        }
        return points;
    }

    voronoi3D(points, bbox) {
        const cells = [];
        for (const p of points) {
            cells.push({ site: p, vertices: [] });
        }

        // For each face, assign to nearest site
        for (const face of this.faces) {
            const centroid = this.faceCentroid(face);
            let minDist = Infinity, bestCell = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < cells.length; i++) {
                const d = this.dist3D(centroid, cells[i].site);
                if (d < minDist) { minDist = d; bestCell = i; }
            }
            cells[bestCell].vertices.push(face);
        }

        return cells;
    }

    faceCentroid(face) {
        let cx = 0, cy = 0, cz = 0;
        for (const idx of face) {
            cx += this.vertices[idx].x;
            cy += this.vertices[idx].y;
            cz += this.vertices[idx].z;
        }
        const n = face.length;
        return { x: cx/n, y: cy/n, z: cz/n };
    }

    dist3D(a, b) {
        return Math.sqrt((a.x-b.x)**2 + (a.y-b.y)**2 + (a.z-b.z)**2);
    }

    fracture(impactPoint, numPieces, radius) {
        const sites = this.generateFracturePoints(impactPoint, numPieces, radius);
        return this.voronoi3D(sites, null);
    }
}

// Example: cube fracture
function cubeFracture() {
    const vertices = [
        {x:0,y:0,z:0},{x:1,y:0,z:0},{x:1,y:1,z:0},{x:0,y:1,z:0},
        {x:0,y:0,z:1},{x:1,y:0,z:1},{x:1,y:1,z:1},{x:0,y:1,z:1}
    ];
    const faces = [[0,1,2,3],[4,5,6,7],[0,1,5,4],[2,3,7,6],[0,3,7,4],[1,2,6,5]];
    const fracture = new FractureVoronoi(vertices, faces);
    return fracture.fracture({x:0.5,y:0.5,z:0.5}, 5, 0.6);
}
Symmetric Matrix Constraint Solving
Solves physics constraints by balancing equations from both sides to prevent ragdoll joints from stretching.
// Symmetric Matrix Constraint Solver (Gauss-Seidel)
class ConstraintSolver {
    constructor(numConstraints) {
        this.numConstraints = numConstraints;
        this.A = Array.from({length: numConstraints}, () => Array(numConstraints).fill(0));
        this.b = Array(numConstraints).fill(0);
        this.x = Array(numConstraints).fill(0);
    }

    setConstraint(i, row, rhs) {
        this.A[i] = [...row];
        this.b[i] = rhs;
    }

    // Symmetric matrix: A[i][j] = A[j][i]
    makeSymmetric() {
        for (let i = 0; i < this.numConstraints; i++) {
            for (let j = i + 1; j < this.numConstraints; j++) {
                const avg = (this.A[i][j] + this.A[j][i]) / 2;
                this.A[i][j] = avg;
                this.A[j][i] = avg;
            }
        }
    }

    // Gauss-Seidel iteration
    solve(iterations = 10) {
        for (let iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) {
            for (let i = 0; i < this.numConstraints; i++) {
                let sum = this.b[i];
                for (let j = 0; j < this.numConstraints; j++) {
                    if (j !== i) sum -= this.A[i][j] * this.x[j];
                }
                if (Math.abs(this.A[i][i]) > 1e-10) {
                    this.x[i] = sum / this.A[i][i];
                }
            }
        }
        return this.x;
    }

    // Project constraints (sequential impulses)
    projectConstraints(constraints, iterations = 10) {
        for (let iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) {
            for (const c of constraints) {
                const {bodyA, bodyB, anchorA, anchorB, stiffness} = c;
                const pA = {
                    x: bodyA.x + anchorA.x,
                    y: bodyA.y + anchorA.y
                };
                const pB = {
                    x: bodyB.x + anchorB.x,
                    y: bodyB.y + anchorB.y
                };
                const dx = pB.x - pA.x;
                const dy = pB.y - pA.y;
                const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
                const target = c.targetLength || 0;
                const error = (dist - target) / dist;

                if (dist > 1e-10) {
                    const cx = dx / dist * error * stiffness;
                    const cy = dy / dist * error * stiffness;
                    bodyA.x += cx * 0.5;
                    bodyA.y += cy * 0.5;
                    bodyB.x -= cx * 0.5;
                    bodyB.y -= cy * 0.5;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// Ragdoll constraint example
function ragdollConstraints() {
    const solver = new ConstraintSolver(4);
    solver.setConstraint(0, [10, -5, 0, 0], 0);
    solver.setConstraint(1, [-5, 10, -5, 0], 0);
    solver.setConstraint(2, [0, -5, 10, -5], 0);
    solver.setConstraint(3, [0, 0, -5, 10], 0);
    solver.makeSymmetric();
    return solver.solve();
}
Signed Distance Field Collision
Checks collisions by sampling a 3D grid of values representing exact distance to closest solid surface.
// Signed Distance Field (SDF)
class SDF {
    constructor(gridSize, bounds) {
        this.gridSize = gridSize;
        this.bounds = bounds;
        this.field = new Float32Array(gridSize ** 3);
        const step = (bounds.max - bounds.min) / gridSize;
        this.step = step;
        this.origin = bounds.min;
    }

    index(x, y, z) {
        return z * this.gridSize * this.gridSize + y * this.gridSize + x;
    }

    sample(x, y, z) {
        const gx = Math.floor((x - this.origin) / this.step);
        const gy = Math.floor((y - this.origin) / this.step);
        const gz = Math.floor((z - this.origin) / this.step);
        if (gx < 0 || gx >= this.gridSize ||
            gy < 0 || gy >= this.gridSize ||
            gz < 0 || gz >= this.gridSize) return Infinity;
        return this.field[this.index(gx, gy, gz)];
    }

    generateSphere(cx, cy, cz, radius) {
        for (let z = 0; z < this.gridSize; z++) {
            for (let y = 0; y < this.gridSize; y++) {
                for (let x = 0; x < this.gridSize; x++) {
                    const wx = this.origin + x * this.step;
                    const wy = this.origin + y * this.step;
                    const wz = this.origin + z * this.step;
                    const dist = Math.sqrt((wx-cx)**2 + (wy-cy)**2 + (wz-cz)**2);
                    this.field[this.index(x,y,z)] = dist - radius;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    collide(px, py, pz) {
        const sdf = this.sample(px, py, pz);
        if (sdf < 0) {
            // Compute gradient for normal
            const eps = this.step * 0.5;
            const nx = (this.sample(px+eps,py,pz) - this.sample(px-eps,py,pz)) / (2*eps);
            const ny = (this.sample(px,py+eps,pz) - this.sample(px,py-eps,pz)) / (2*eps);
            const nz = (this.sample(px,py,pz+eps) - this.sample(px,py,pz-eps)) / (2*eps);
            const len = Math.sqrt(nx*nx+ny*ny+nz*nz);
            return {
                inside: true,
                depth: -sdf,
                normal: {x: nx/len, y: ny/len, z: nz/len}
            };
        }
        return {inside: false};
    }

    collideSDF(other, otherSDF) {
        const results = [];
        for (let z = 0; z < otherSDF.gridSize; z++) {
            for (let y = 0; y < otherSDF.gridSize; y++) {
                for (let x = 0; x < otherSDF.gridSize; x++) {
                    const wx = otherSDF.origin + x * otherSDF.step;
                    const wy = otherSDF.origin + y * otherSDF.step;
                    const wz = otherSDF.origin + z * otherSDF.step;
                    const result = this.collide(wx, wy, wz);
                    if (result.inside) results.push({point: {x: wx, y: wy, z: wz}, ...result});
                }
            }
        }
        return results;
    }
}

// SDF collision check
function sdfCollision() {
    const sdf = new SDF(32, {min: -2, max: 2});
    sdf.generateSphere(0, 0, 0, 1);
    return sdf.collide(0.5, 0.5, 0.5);
}
Capsule-Capsule Intersection Math
Finds the shortest line segment between two 3D capsule lines for character-to-character body collisions.
// Capsule-Capsule Intersection
class Capsule {
    constructor(a, b, radius) {
        this.a = a; // start point
        this.b = b; // end point
        this.radius = radius;
    }
}

function closestPointsBetweenSegments(a1, a2, b1, b2) {
    const d1 = {x: a2.x-a1.x, y: a2.y-a1.y, z: a2.z-a1.z};
    const d2 = {x: b2.x-b1.x, y: b2.y-b1.y, z: b2.z-b1.z};
    const r = {x: a1.x-b1.x, y: a1.y-b1.y, z: a1.z-b1.z};

    const dotD1D1 = d1.x*d1.x + d1.y*d1.y + d1.z*d1.z;
    const dotD2D2 = d2.x*d2.x + d2.y*d2.y + d2.z*d2.z;
    const dotD1D2 = d1.x*d2.x + d1.y*d2.y + d1.z*d2.z;
    const dotD1R = d1.x*r.x + d1.y*r.y + d1.z*r.z;
    const dotD2R = d2.x*r.x + d2.y*r.y + d2.z*r.z;

    const denom = dotD1D1 * dotD2D2 - dotD1D2 * dotD1D2;

    let s, t;
    if (Math.abs(denom) > 1e-10) {
        s = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (dotD1D2 * dotD2R - dotD2D2 * dotD1R) / denom));
        t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (dotD1D2 * dotD1R - dotD1D1 * dotD2R) / denom));
    } else {
        s = 0; t = 0;
    }

    // Closest points
    const pA = {
        x: a1.x + s * d1.x,
        y: a1.y + s * d1.y,
        z: a1.z + s * d1.z
    };
    const pB = {
        x: b1.x + t * d2.x,
        y: b1.y + t * d2.y,
        z: b1.z + t * d2.z
    };

    return {pA, pB, distance: Math.sqrt((pA.x-pB.x)**2 + (pA.y-pB.y)**2 + (pA.z-pB.z)**2)};
}

function capsuleIntersect(c1, c2) {
    const closest = closestPointsBetweenSegments(c1.a, c1.b, c2.a, c2.b);
    const combinedRadius = c1.radius + c2.radius;
    return {
        collides: closest.distance <= combinedRadius,
        depth: combinedRadius - closest.distance,
        contactA: closest.pA,
        contactB: closest.pB,
        normal: closest.distance > 0 ? {
            x: (closest.pB.x - closest.pA.x) / closest.distance,
            y: (closest.pB.y - closest.pA.y) / closest.distance,
            z: (closest.pB.z - closest.pA.z) / closest.distance
        } : {x: 0, y: 1, z: 0}
    };
}

// Example
function capsuleCollisionDemo() {
    const c1 = new Capsule({x:0,y:0,z:0}, {x:0,y:1,z:0}, 0.5);
    const c2 = new Capsule({x:0.5,y:0,z:0}, {x:0.5,y:1,z:0}, 0.5);
    return capsuleIntersect(c1, c2);
}
Cloth Shear/Bend Spring Integration
Connects cloth vertices diagonally and across gaps with mathematical springs to prevent fabric stretching.
// Cloth Simulation with Springs
class ClothSimulation {
    constructor(width, height, spacing) {
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
        this.spacing = spacing;
        this.particles = [];
        this.constraints = [];
        this.gravity = {x: 0, y: 9.81, z: 0};
        this.damping = 0.99;

        // Initialize grid of particles
        for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
            for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {
                this.particles.push({
                    x: x * spacing, y: y * spacing, z: 0,
                    vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0,
                    pinned: y === 0 // Pin top row
                });
            }
        }

        // Create springs
        for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
            for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {
                const i = y * width + x;

                // Structural springs (horizontal & vertical)
                if (x < width - 1) this.addSpring(i, i + 1, spacing, 800);
                if (y < height - 1) this.addSpring(i, i + width, spacing, 800);

                // Shear springs (diagonals)
                if (x < width - 1 && y < height - 1) {
                    this.addSpring(i, i + width + 1, spacing * Math.SQRT2, 600);
                }
                if (x > 0 && y < height - 1) {
                    this.addSpring(i, i + width - 1, spacing * Math.SQRT2, 600);
                }

                // Bend springs (skip one)
                if (x < width - 2) this.addSpring(i, i + 2, spacing * 2, 200);
                if (y < height - 2) this.addSpring(i, i + width * 2, spacing * 2, 200);
            }
        }
    }

    addSpring(i, j, restLength, stiffness) {
        this.constraints.push({i, j, restLength, stiffness});
    }

    step(dt) {
        // Apply gravity
        for (const p of this.particles) {
            if (p.pinned) continue;
            p.vy += this.gravity.y * dt;
        }

        // Solve spring constraints (iterative)
        for (let iter = 0; iter < 5; iter++) {
            for (const c of this.constraints) {
                const pA = this.particles[c.i];
                const pB = this.particles[c.j];

                const dx = pB.x - pA.x;
                const dy = pB.y - pA.y;
                const dz = pB.z - pA.z;
                const dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz);

                if (dist > 0) {
                    const error = (dist - c.restLength) / dist;
                    const stiffness = c.stiffness * 0.5;
                    const cx = dx * error * stiffness;
                    const cy = dy * error * stiffness;
                    const cz = dz * error * stiffness;

                    if (!pA.pinned) { pA.x += cx; pA.y += cy; pA.z += cz; }
                    if (!pB.pinned) { pB.x -= cx; pB.y -= cy; pB.z -= cz; }
                }
            }
        }

        // Integrate velocity
        for (const p of this.particles) {
            if (p.pinned) continue;
            p.x += p.vx * dt;
            p.y += p.vy * dt;
            p.z += p.vz * dt;
            p.vx *= this.damping;
            p.vy *= this.damping;
            p.vz *= this.damping;
        }
    }

    getMesh() {
        const mesh = [];
        for (let y = 0; y < this.height - 1; y++) {
            for (let x = 0; x < this.width - 1; x++) {
                const i = y * this.width + x;
                mesh.push([
                    this.particles[i], this.particles[i+1],
                    this.particles[i+this.width+1], this.particles[i+this.width]
                ]);
            }
        }
        return mesh;
    }
}

function clothDemo() {
    const cloth = new ClothSimulation(10, 10, 0.1);
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) cloth.step(0.016);
    return cloth.getMesh().length;
}

Project Euler #1-6
Multiples, Fibonacci, Primes, Palindromes, LCM, Sum-Square.
// 1: Multiples of 3 or 5
function multiples35(n) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (i % 3 === 0 || i % 5 === 0) sum += i;
    }
    return sum;
}

// 2: Even Fibonacci Numbers
function evenFib(limit) {
    let a = 1, b = 2, sum = 0;
    while (b <= limit) {
        if (b % 2 === 0) sum += b;
        [a, b] = [b, a + b];
    }
    return sum;
}

// 3: Largest Prime Factor
function largestPrimeFactor(n) {
    let largest = 0;
    for (let i = 2; i * i <= n; i++) {
        while (n % i === 0) { largest = i; n /= i; }
    }
    return n > largest ? n : largest;
}

// 4: Largest Palindrome Product
function largestPalindrome(n) {
    let max = 0;
    for (let i = Math.pow(10, n) - 1; i >= Math.pow(10, n - 1); i--) {
        for (let j = i; j >= Math.pow(10, n - 1); j--) {
            const prod = i * j;
            const s = prod.toString();
            if (s === s.split('').reverse().join('') && prod > max) max = prod;
        }
    }
    return max;
}

// 5: Smallest Multiple
function smallestMultiple(n) {
    let lcm = 1;
    for (let i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
        lcm = (lcm * i) / gcd(lcm, i);
    }
    return lcm;
}

// 6: Sum Square Difference
function sumSquareDiff(n) {
    let sumSq = 0, sqSum = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) { sumSq += i * i; sqSum += i; }
    return sqSum * sqSum - sumSq;
}

function gcd(a, b) { return b === 0 ? a : gcd(b, a % b); }
Project Euler #7-12
Primes, Products, Pythagorean, Triangular, Grid.
// 7: 10001st Prime
function nthPrime(n) {
    const primes = [2];
    let num = 3;
    while (primes.length < n) {
        let isPrime = true;
        for (const p of primes) {
            if (p * p > num) break;
            if (num % p === 0) { isPrime = false; break; }
        }
        if (isPrime) primes.push(num);
        num += 2;
    }
    return primes[n - 1];
}

// 8: Largest Product in a Series
function largestProduct(n) {
    const digits = '73167176531330624919225119674426574742355349194934969835203127745063262395783180169848018694788518438586156078911294949545959895554435246381423602679471039014718821934165320965311421040860940447302645369181042529668685146338093908791204634272518018349898151251906960064887469744525184750364678165944860575540926490432094572572856831623434410693068981415506965728997473545764094608203548112096244993359347639241499344305561049649866627363629121938578200387592463168894671414666651641855978709616072258192651086251268886093084706166413747610485541826386327135438748527461509507996944963088339093974048700638131742597943804442932543272324749195873643065919458371561651209829252202041549369276824734383759014486676996291113839392336086457645934589288812819238123163527491405313196565156612284721063346666616855885499565999932356845070698749632902281396874329118726626137946879405961354882831280649389340328808046149000654109549931245418947023616244019035300751430928255408924325345364753253637394755464101814620063110793729006132764263520229541624767175696324523446325411059346497798606938750681016616242008830888546866340922839540427941820832034384144847699034126551157709351002141497808569302299309411129206673020745795802078013852309648672098037420398997468288749790887868240376375017581587003277366721586201334092568154351249702148417256020945044741301806587004914928715010237033125713507689632344009303391280980971348085869326808383755034047692232627453561236614405866098568511585633950705425012625222448736207857740510047354487312801968709990543280723226507787552547490762366195577683327804517743713845361903208994670896041893768023280842982553913928459815914075223908824498889436915527668673150533370065580341895595806639603995089068976584155306335034230873191036696908567710371910643419027425108144354747106250338354875953257305500528016312027198820393046106280571467084315412533548846880662855786728000001';
    return parseInt(digits.substring(0, n));
}

// 9: Special Pythagorean Triplet
function pythagoreanTriplet(sum) {
    for (let a = 1; a < sum / 3; a++) {
        for (let b = a + 1; b < sum / 2; b++) {
            const c = sum - a - b;
            if (a * a + b * b === c * c) return { a, b, c, product: a * b * c };
        }
    }
    return null;
}

// 10: Summation of Primes
function sumPrimes(n) {
    const sieve = new Array(n + 1).fill(true);
    sieve[0] = sieve[1] = false;
    for (let i = 2; i * i <= n; i++) {
        if (sieve[i]) for (let j = i * i; j <= n; j += i) sieve[j] = false;
    }
    return sieve.reduce((sum, val, idx) => val ? sum + idx : sum, 0);
}

// 11: Largest Product in a Grid
function largestGridProduct(grid) {
    let max = 0;
    for (let r = 0; r < 20; r++) {
        for (let c = 0; c < 20; c++) {
            // Horizontal
            if (c + 3 < 20) { const p = grid[r][c]*grid[r][c+1]*grid[r][c+2]*grid[r][c+3]; if(p>max) max=p; }
            // Vertical
            if (r + 3 < 20) { const p = grid[r][c]*grid[r+1][c]*grid[r+2][c]*grid[r+3][c]; if(p>max) max=p; }
            // Diagonal
            if (r + 3 < 20 && c + 3 < 20) { const p = grid[r][c]*grid[r+1][c+1]*grid[r+2][c+2]*grid[r+3][c+3]; if(p>max) max=p; }
        }
    }
    return max;
}

// 12: Highly Divisible Triangular Number
function highlyDivisibleTriangular(n) {
    let tri = 0, num = 1;
    while (true) {
        tri += num++;
        let divisors = 0;
        for (let i = 1; i * i <= tri; i++) {
            if (tri % i === 0) divisors += i * i === tri ? 1 : 2;
        }
        if (divisors > n) return tri;
    }
}
Project Euler #13-18
Large sums, Collatz, Lattice, Powers, Letters, Paths.
// 13: Large Sum (first 10 digits)
function largeSum(digits) {
    let sum = BigInt(0);
    for (const d of digits) sum += BigInt(d);
    return sum.toString().substring(0, 10);
}

// 14: Longest Collatz Sequence
function longestCollatz(limit) {
    const cache = { 1: 1 };
    function collatzLen(n) {
        if (cache[n]) return cache[n];
        const next = n % 2 === 0 ? n / 2 : 3 * n + 1;
        cache[n] = 1 + collatzLen(next);
        return cache[n];
    }
    let maxLen = 0, result = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < limit; i++) {
        const len = collatzLen(i);
        if (len > maxLen) { maxLen = len; result = i; }
    }
    return result;
}

// 15: Lattice Paths (combinatorics)
function latticePaths(n) {
    let paths = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        paths = paths * (n + i + 1) / (i + 1);
    }
    return Math.round(paths);
}

// 16: Power Digit Sum
function powerDigitSum(exp) {
    let num = BigInt(1);
    for (let i = 0; i < exp; i++) num *= BigInt(2);
    return num.toString().split('').reduce((s, d) => s + parseInt(d), 0);
}

// 17: Number Letter Counts
function numberLetterCounts(n) {
    const ones = [0,3,3,5,4,4,3,5,5,4];
    const tens = [0,3,6,6,5,5,5,7,6,6];
    const hundred = 7, and = 3, thousand = 8;
    let count = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        const h = Math.floor(i / 100);
        const t = Math.floor((i % 100) / 10);
        const o = i % 10;
        if (h > 0) { count += ones[h] + hundred; if (t > 0 || o > 0) count += and; }
        if (t === 1) count += tens[1] + ones[o];
        else { count += tens[t] + ones[o]; }
    }
    return count;
}

// 18: Maximum Path Sum I
function maxPathSum(triangle) {
    for (let row = triangle.length - 2; row >= 0; row--) {
        for (let col = 0; col < triangle[row].length; col++) {
            triangle[row][col] += Math.max(triangle[row+1][col], triangle[row+1][col+1]);
        }
    }
    return triangle[0][0];
}
Project Euler #19-25
Sundays, Factorials, Amicables, Permutations, Fibonacci.
// 19: Counting Sundays
function countingSundays() {
    let count = 0;
    for (let y = 1901; y <= 2000; y++) {
        for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {
            const d = new Date(y, m - 1, 1);
            if (d.getDay() === 0) count++;
        }
    }
    return count;
}

// 20: Factorial Digit Sum
function factorialDigitSum(n) {
    let fact = BigInt(1);
    for (let i = 2; i <= n; i++) fact *= BigInt(i);
    return fact.toString().split('').reduce((s, d) => s + parseInt(d), 0);
}

// 21: Amicable Numbers
function amicableSum(limit) {
    function d(n) {
        let sum = 1;
        for (let i = 2; i * i <= n; i++) { if (n % i === 0) sum += i + (i !== n/i ? n/i : 0); }
        return sum;
    }
    let sum = 0;
    for (let a = 2; a < limit; a++) {
        const b = d(a);
        if (b !== a && d(b) === a) sum += a;
    }
    return sum;
}

// 23: Non-abundant Sums
function nonAbundantSums(limit) {
    function d(n) {
        let sum = 1;
        for (let i = 2; i * i <= n; i++) { if (n % i === 0) sum += i + (i !== n/i ? n/i : 0); }
        return sum;
    }
    const abundant = [];
    for (let i = 2; i < limit; i++) { if (d(i) > i) abundant.push(i); }
    const canBeWritten = new Set();
    for (let i = 0; i < abundant.length; i++) {
        for (let j = i; j < abundant.length; j++) {
            const sum = abundant[i] + abundant[j];
            if (sum < limit) canBeWritten.add(sum);
            else break;
        }
    }
    let total = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < limit; i++) { if (!canBeWritten.has(i)) total += i; }
    return total;
}

// 24: Lexicographic Permutations
function lexicographicPermutation(n, digits) {
    let remaining = [...digits];
    let result = '';
    let rank = n - 1;
    for (let i = digits.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const factorial = Array.from({length: i + 1}, (_, k) => k ? factorial(k) : 1)[i];
        const idx = Math.floor(rank / factorial);
        result += remaining[idx];
        remaining.splice(idx, 1);
        rank %= factorial;
    }
    return result;
}
function factorial(n) { return n <= 1 ? 1 : n * factorial(n - 1); }

// 25: 1000-digit Fibonacci Number
function thousandDigitFib() {
    let a = BigInt(1), b = BigInt(1), idx = 2;
    while (b.toString().length < 1000) { [a, b] = [b, a + b]; idx++; }
    return idx;
}
Project Euler #26-31
Reciprocal Cycles, Quadratic Primes, Spiral Diagonals, Powers, Digit Powers, Coin Sums.
// 26: Reciprocal Cycles
function reciprocalCycles(n) {
    let maxLen = 0, result = 0;
    for (let d = 2; d < n; d++) {
        const seen = {};
        let rem = 1, pos = 0;
        while (rem !== 0 && !(rem in seen)) { seen[rem] = pos++; rem = (rem * 10) % d; }
        const len = rem !== 0 ? pos - seen[rem] : 0;
        if (len > maxLen) { maxLen = len; result = d; }
    }
    return result;
}

// 27: Quadratic Primes
function quadraticPrimes() {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    let max = 0, best = 0;
    for (let a = -999; a < 1000; a++) {
        for (let b = -1000; b <= 1000; b++) {
            let n = 0;
            while (isPrime(n*n + a*n + b)) n++;
            if (n > max) { max = n; best = a * b; }
        }
    }
    return best;
}

// 28: Number Spiral Diagonals
function spiralDiagonals(n) {
    let sum = 1, num = 1;
    for (let i = 3; i <= n; i += 2) {
        for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) { num += i - 1; sum += num; }
    }
    return sum;
}

// 29: Distinct Powers
function distinctPowers(n) {
    const s = new Set();
    for (let a = 2; a <= n; a++) {
        let val = BigInt(a);
        for (let b = 2; b <= n; b++) { val *= BigInt(a); s.add(val.toString()); }
    }
    return s.size;
}

// 30: Digit Fifth Powers
function digitFifthPowers() {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 2; i < 354294; i++) {
        const s = i.toString().split('').reduce((acc, d) => acc + Math.pow(parseInt(d), 5), 0);
        if (s === i) sum += i;
    }
    return sum;
}

// 31: Coin Sums (DP)
function coinSums(target) {
    const coins = [1, 2, 5, 10, 20, 50, 100, 200];
    const ways = new Array(target + 1).fill(0);
    ways[0] = 1;
    for (const coin of coins) {
        for (let i = coin; i <= target; i++) ways[i] += ways[i - coin];
    }
    return ways[target];
}
Project Euler #32-37
Pandigital, Digit Cancelling, Circular Primes, Truncatable, Champernowne.
// 32: Pandigital Products
function pandigitalProducts() {
    const products = new Set();
    for (let i = 1; i < 100; i++) {
        for (let j = 1; j < 10000; j++) {
            const prod = i * j;
            const s = (i.toString() + j.toString() + prod.toString());
            if (s.length === 9 && new Set(s).size === 9 && !s.includes('0')) products.add(prod);
        }
    }
    return [...products].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
}

// 33: Digit Cancelling Fractions
function digitCancelling() {
    let num = 1, den = 1;
    for (let i = 10; i < 100; i++) {
        for (let j = i + 1; j < 100; j++) {
            const si = i.toString(), sj = j.toString();
            for (let d = 0; d < 2; d++) {
                for (let n = 0; n < 2; n++) {
                    if (si[d] === sj[n]) {
                        const ni = parseInt(si[1 - d]), nj = parseInt(sj[1 - n]);
                        if (nj !== 0 && i * nj === j * ni) { num *= ni; den *= nj; }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return den / gcd(num, den);
}

// 34: Digit Factorials
function digitFactorials() {
    const facts = [1,1,2,6,24,120,720,5040,40320,362880];
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 3; i < 50000; i++) {
        const s = i.toString().split('').reduce((a, d) => a + facts[parseInt(d)], 0);
        if (s === i) sum += i;
    }
    return sum;
}

// 35: Circular Primes
function circularPrimes(n) {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    let count = 0;
    for (let i = 2; i < n; i++) {
        const s = i.toString();
        let circ = true;
        for (let j = 0; j < s.length; j++) {
            const rotated = parseInt(s.substring(j) + s.substring(0, j));
            if (!isPrime(rotated)) { circ = false; break; }
        }
        if (circ) count++;
    }
    return count;
}

// 36: Double-base Palindromes
function doubleBasePalindromes(n) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {
        const dec = i.toString(), bin = i.toString(2);
        if (dec === dec.split('').reverse().join('') && bin === bin.split('').reverse().join('')) sum += i;
    }
    return sum;
}

// 37: Truncatable Primes
function truncatablePrimes() {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    const primes = [];
    for (let i = 11; primes.length < 11; i++) {
        if (!isPrime(i)) continue;
        const s = i.toString();
        let trunc = true;
        for (let j = 1; j < s.length; j++) {
            if (!isPrime(parseInt(s.substring(j))) || !isPrime(parseInt(s.substring(0, s.length - j)))) { trunc = false; break; }
        }
        if (trunc) primes.push(i);
    }
    return primes.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
}

function gcd(a, b) { return b === 0 ? a : gcd(b, a % b); }
Project Euler #38-43
Pandigital Multiples, Integer Triangles, Champernowne, Pandigital Prime, Coded Triangles, Sub-string Divisibility.
// 38: Pandigital Multiples
function pandigitalMultiples() {
    let max = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < 10000; i++) {
        let s = '';
        for (let j = 1; s.length < 10; j++) s += i * j;
        if (s.length === 9 && new Set(s).size === 9 && !s.includes('0')) max = Math.max(max, parseInt(s));
    }
    return max;
}

// 39: Integer Right Triangles
function integerRightTriangles(limit) {
    const counts = new Array(limit + 1).fill(0);
    for (let a = 1; a < limit; a++) {
        for (let b = a; b < limit; b++) {
            const c = Math.sqrt(a*a + b*b);
            if (c === Math.floor(c) && a + b + c <= limit) counts[a + b + c]++;
        }
    }
    return counts.indexOf(Math.max(...counts));
}

// 40: Champernowne's Constant
function champernowne(n) {
    let s = '', i = 1;
    while (s.length < n) s += i++;
    return [1,10,100,1000,10000,100000,1000000].reduce((p, idx) => p * parseInt(s[idx - 1]), 1);
}

// 41: Pandigital Prime
function pandigitalPrime() {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    const digits = '7654321';
    for (let i = 0; i < 5040; i++) {
        const num = parseInt(permute(i, digits));
        if (isPrime(num)) return num;
    }
    return 0;
}
function permute(idx, s) {
    const arr = s.split('');
    for (let i = arr.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = idx % (i + 1); idx = Math.floor(idx / (i + 1));
        [arr[i], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[i]];
    }
    return arr.join('');
}

// 42: Coded Triangle Numbers
function codedTriangleNumbers(words) {
    const triangles = new Set();
    for (let n = 1; n <= 20; n++) triangles.add(n * (n + 1) / 2);
    let count = 0;
    for (const w of words) {
        const value = w.split('').reduce((s, c) => s + c.charCodeAt(0) - 64, 0);
        if (triangles.has(value)) count++;
    }
    return count;
}

// 43: Sub-string Divisibility
function subStringDivisibility() {
    const primes = [2,3,5,7,11,13,17];
    const perms = [];
    function gen(s, used) {
        if (s.length === 10) { perms.push(s); return; }
        for (let i = 0; i <= 9; i++) {
            if (!used.has(i) && (s.length < 3 || parseInt(s.substring(s.length-2)) % primes[s.length-3] === 0)) {
                used.add(i); gen(s + i, used); used.delete(i);
            }
        }
    }
    gen('', new Set());
    return perms.reduce((a, b) => a + BigInt(b), BigInt(0)).toString();
}
Project Euler #44-50
Pentagon Numbers, Goldbach, Distinct Primes, Self Powers, Prime Permutations.
// 44: Pentagon Numbers
function pentagonNumbers() {
    const pent = (n) => n * (3*n - 1) / 2;
    const pSet = new Set();
    for (let i = 1; i < 10000; i++) { pSet.add(pent(i)); }
    for (let j = 1; j < 2500; j++) {
        for (let k = j + 1; k < 2500; k++) {
            const pj = pent(j), pk = pent(k);
            if (pSet.has(pj + pk) && pSet.has(pk - pj)) return pk - pj;
        }
    }
}

// 45: Triangular, Pentagonal, Hexagonal
function triPentHex() {
    const isPent = (n) => { const x = (Math.sqrt(24*n+1)+1)/6; return x === Math.floor(x); };
    for (let n = 286; ; n++) {
        const h = n*(2*n-1);
        if (isPent(h)) return h;
    }
}

// 46: Goldbach's Other Conjecture
function goldbachOther() {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    for (let n = 9; ; n += 2) {
        if (isPrime(n)) continue;
        let found = false;
        for (let i = 1; 2*i*i < n; i++) { if (isPrime(n - 2*i*i)) { found = true; break; } }
        if (!found) return n;
    }
}

// 47: Distinct Primes Factors
function distinctPrimesFactors(n) {
    function countFactors(num) {
        const factors = new Set();
        for (let i = 2; i * i <= num; i++) { while (num % i === 0) { factors.add(i); num /= i; } }
        if (num > 1) factors.add(num);
        return factors.size;
    }
    let count = 0;
    for (let i = 2; count < n; i++) {
        if (countFactors(i) === n) count++;
        else count = 0;
        if (count === n) return i - n + 1;
    }
}

// 48: Self Powers
function selfPowers(n) {
    let sum = BigInt(0);
    for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        let pow = BigInt(1);
        for (let j = 0; j < i; j++) pow = (pow * BigInt(i)) % BigInt(10**10);
        sum = (sum + pow) % BigInt(10**10);
    }
    return sum.toString();
}

// 49: Prime Permutations
function primePermutations() {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    for (let i = 1489; i < 10000; i += 2) {
        if (!isPrime(i)) continue;
        const perms = new Set();
        const s = i.toString().split('').sort().join('');
        for (let j = i + 2; j < 10000; j += 2) {
            if (isPrime(j) && j.toString().split('').sort().join('') === s) perms.add(j);
        }
        for (const p of perms) { const q = 2*p - i; if (perms.has(q) && isPrime(q)) return `${i}${p}${q}`; }
    }
}

// 50: Consecutive Prime Sum
function consecutivePrimeSum(limit) {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    const primes = [];
    for (let i = 2; i < limit; i++) { if (isPrime(i)) primes.push(i); }
    let maxLen = 0, maxSum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < primes.length; i++) {
        let sum = 0;
        for (let j = i; j < primes.length; j++) {
            sum += primes[j];
            if (sum >= limit) break;
            if (j - i + 1 > maxLen && isPrime(sum)) { maxLen = j - i + 1; maxSum = sum; }
        }
    }
    return maxSum;
}
Project Euler #51-60
Prime Replacements, Permuted Multiples, Poker, Lychrel, Spiral Primes, XOR, Prime Pairs.
// 51: Prime Digit Replacements
function primeDigitReplacements() {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    for (let n = 1; ; n++) {
        const s = n.toString();
        for (let mask = 1; mask < (1 << s.length); mask++) {
            const family = [];
            for (let d = 0; d <= 9; d++) {
                let ns = '';
                for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) ns += (mask & (1 << i)) ? d : s[i];
                if (ns[0] !== '0' && isPrime(parseInt(ns))) family.push(parseInt(ns));
            }
            if (family.length === 8) return family[0];
        }
    }
}

// 52: Permuted Multiples
function permutedMultiples() {
    for (let i = 1; ; i++) {
        const s = i.toString().split('').sort().join('');
        let ok = true;
        for (let j = 2; j <= 6; j++) {
            if ((i * j).toString().split('').sort().join('') !== s) { ok = false; break; }
        }
        if (ok) return i;
    }
}

// 53: Combinatoric Selections
function combinatoricSelections() {
    let count = 0;
    for (let n = 1; n <= 100; n++) {
        for (let r = 1; r <= n; r++) {
            let c = 1;
            for (let i = 0; i < r; i++) c = c * (n - i) / (i + 1);
            if (c > 1000000) count++;
        }
    }
    return count;
}

// 55: Lychrel Numbers
function lychrelNumbers(limit) {
    function reverse(n) { return parseInt(n.toString().split('').reverse().join('')); }
    let count = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < limit; i++) {
        let n = i, ly = true;
        for (let j = 0; j < 50; j++) { n += reverse(n); if (n.toString() === n.toString().split('').reverse().join('')) { ly = false; break; } }
        if (ly) count++;
    }
    return count;
}

// 56: Powerful Digit Sum
function powerfulDigitSum() {
    let max = 0;
    for (let a = 1; a < 100; a++) {
        for (let b = 1; b < 100; b++) {
            const sum = BigInt(a)**BigInt(b).toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s+parseInt(d),0);
            if (sum > max) max = sum;
        }
    }
    return max;
}

// 57: Square Root Convergents
function squareRootConvergents(n) {
    let num = BigInt(3), den = BigInt(2), count = 0;
    for (let i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
        [num, den] = [num + 2n * den, num + den];
        if (num.toString().length > den.toString().length) count++;
    }
    return count;
}

// 58: Spiral Primes
function spiralPrimes() {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    let primes = 0, total = 1;
    for (let side = 3; ; side += 2) {
        const sq = side * side;
        for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) { if (isPrime(sq - j * (side - 1))) primes++; }
        total += 4;
        if (primes / total < 0.1) return side;
    }
}

// 59: XOR Decryption
function xorDecryption(cipher) {
    let maxScore = 0, bestKey = '';
    for (let a = 97; a <= 122; a++) {
        for (let b = 97; b <= 122; b++) {
            for (let c = 97; c <= 122; c++) {
                const key = [a, b, c];
                let score = 0;
                const decoded = cipher.map((ch, i) => {
                    const d = ch ^ key[i % 3];
                    if (d >= 32 && d <= 122) score++;
                    return d;
                });
                if (score > maxScore) { maxScore = score; bestKey = String.fromCharCode(a,b,c); }
            }
        }
    }
    return cipher.reduce((sum, ch, i) => sum + (ch ^ bestKey.charCodeAt(i % 3)), 0);
}

// 60: Prime Pair Sets
function primePairSets() {
    function isPrime(n) { if (n < 2) return false; for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false; return true; }
    function concat(a, b) { return parseInt(a.toString() + b.toString()); }
    function formsPair(a, b) { return isPrime(concat(a, b)) && isPrime(concat(b, a)); }
    const primes = [];
    for (let i = 2; primes.length < 100; i++) { if (isPrime(i)) primes.push(i); }
    for (let a = 0; a < primes.length; a++) {
        for (let b = a + 1; b < primes.length; b++) {
            if (!formsPair(primes[a], primes[b])) continue;
            for (let c = b + 1; c < primes.length; c++) {
                if (!formsPair(primes[a], primes[c]) || !formsPair(primes[b], primes[c])) continue;
                for (let d = c + 1; d < primes.length; d++) {
                    if (!formsPair(primes[a], primes[d]) || !formsPair(primes[b], primes[d]) || !formsPair(primes[c], primes[d])) continue;
                    for (let e = d + 1; e < primes.length; e++) {
                        if (formsPair(primes[a], primes[e]) && formsPair(primes[b], primes[e]) && formsPair(primes[c], primes[e]) && formsPair(primes[d], primes[e])) {
                            return primes[a] + primes[b] + primes[c] + primes[d] + primes[e];
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// 61: Cyclical Figurate Numbers
function cyclicalFigurate() {
    function figurate(type, n) {
        if (type===3) return n*(n+1)/2;
        if (type===4) return n*n;
        if (type===5) return n*(3*n-1)/2;
        if (type===6) return n*(2*n-1);
        if (type===7) return n*(5*n-3)/2;
        if (type===8) return n*(3*n-2);
    }
    const byPrefix = {};
    for (let t = 3; t <= 8; t++) {
        for (let n = 1; ; n++) {
            const v = figurate(t, n);
            if (v > 9999) break;
            if (v > 999) {
                const p = Math.floor(v/100), s = v%100;
                if (!byPrefix[p]) byPrefix[p] = [];
                byPrefix[p].push({type:t, suffix:s, val:v});
            }
        }
    }
    for (const start of Object.values(byPrefix).flat()) {
        const chain = [start];
        const used = new Set([start.type]);
        let curr = start.suffix;
        for (let len = 1; len < 6; len++) {
            const next = (byPrefix[curr]||[]).find(x=>!used.has(x.type));
            if (!next) break;
            chain.push(next); used.add(next.type); curr = next.suffix;
        }
        if (chain.length === 6 && chain[5].suffix === start.val%100) {
            return chain.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.val, 0);
        }
    }
}

// 62: Cubic Permutations
function cubicPermutations() {
    function isPerm(a,b) { return a.toString().split('').sort().join('')===b.toString().split('').sort().join(''); }
    for (let n = 1; ; n++) {
        const cube = n*n*n, perms = [];
        for (let m = 1; m <= n*2; m++) { if (isPerm(cube, m*m*m)) perms.push(m*m*m); }
        if (perms.length === 5) return perms[0];
    }
}

// 63: Powerful Digit Counts
function powerfulDigitCounts() {
    let count = 0;
    for (let n = 1; n <= 22; n++) {
        for (let b = 1; b <= 9; b++) {
            if (BigInt(b)**BigInt(n).toString().length === n) count++;
        }
    }
    return count;
}

// 64: Odd Period Square Roots
function oddPeriodRoots(limit) {
    function period(n) {
        const m0 = Math.floor(Math.sqrt(n));
        if (m0*m0===n) return 0;
        let a=m0,d=1,m=a,p=0;
        while(a!==2*m0) { d=(n-m*m)/d; a=Math.floor((m0+m)/d); m=d*a-m; p++; }
        return p;
    }
    let count=0;
    for(let n=2;n<=limit;n++) if(period(n)%2===1) count++;
    return count;
}

// 65: Convergents of e
function convergentsOfE(n) {
    let num=BigInt(1),den=BigInt(0);
    const terms=[2];
    for(let k=1;k<=n/3;k++) { terms.push(1); terms.push(2*k); terms.push(1); }
    for(let i=terms.length-1;i>=0;i--) {
        [num,den]=[den,num+den*BigInt(terms[i])];
    }
    return num.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s+parseInt(d),0);
}

// 66: Diophantine Equation (Pell)
function diophantine(limit) {
    function isSq(n) { const s=Math.floor(Math.sqrt(n)); return s*s===n; }
    let maxX=0, bestD=0;
    for(let D=2;D<=limit;D++) {
        if(isSq(D)) continue;
        let m=0,d=1,a=Math.floor(Math.sqrt(D));
        let num=BigInt(a),den=BigInt(1),prevNum=BigInt(1),prevDen=BigInt(0);
        while(num*num-BigInt(D)*den*den!==1n) {
            m=d*a-m; d=(D-m*m)/d; a=Math.floor((Math.floor(Math.sqrt(D))+m)/d);
            const tmp=num; num=BigInt(a)*num+prevNum; prevNum=tmp;
            const tmp2=den; den=BigInt(a)*den+prevDen; prevDen=tmp2;
        }
        if(num>maxX) { maxX=num; bestD=D; }
    }
    return bestD;
}

}

// 71: Ordered Fractions
function orderedFractions(limit) {
    let bestN=0,bestD=1;
    for(let d=limit;d>2;d--) {
        const n=Math.floor(d*3/7);
        if(n*7===3*d) continue;
        if(3*d>7*n && 7*n*bestD>bestN*7*d) { bestN=n; bestD=d; }
    }
    return bestN;
}

// 72: Counting Fractions
function countingFractions(n) {
    const phi=new Array(n+1).fill(0).map((_,i)=>i);
    for(let i=2;i<=n;i++) if(phi[i]===i) for(let j=i;j<=n;j+=i) phi[j]-=phi[j]/i;
    return phi.reduce((s,v,i)=>i>=2?s+v:s,0);
}

// 73: Counting Fractions in Range
function fractionsInRange(n) {
    let count=0;
    for(let d=2;d<=n;d++) {
        const lo=Math.floor(d/3)+1, hi=Math.floor(d/2);
        for(let i=lo;i<=hi;i++) if(gcd(i,d)===1) count++;
    }
    return count;
}

}

// 78: Coin Partitions
function coinPartitions() {
    const p=[1];let n=0;
    while(true) { n++;let s=0;
        for(let k=1;;k++) { const g1=k*(3*k-1)/2,g2=k*(3*k+1)/2;
            if(g1>n)break;const sign=k%2===0?-1:1;
            s+=sign*p[n-g1];if(g2<=n) s+=sign*p[n-g2];
        }
        p[n]=s; if(p[n]%1e6===0) return n;
    }
}

}

// 81: Path Sum Two Ways
function pathSumTwoWays(matrix) {
    const n=matrix.length;
    const dp=Array.from({length:n},()=>Array(n).fill(Infinity));
    dp[0][0]=matrix[0][0];
    for(let i=0;i0) dp[i][j]=Math.min(dp[i][j],dp[i-1][j]+matrix[i][j]);
        if(j>0) dp[i][j]=Math.min(dp[i][j],dp[i][j-1]+matrix[i][j]);
    }
    return dp[n-1][n-1];
}

// 90: Cube Digit Pairs
function cubeDigitPairs() {
    const digits='0123456789';
    function valid(a,b) {
        const needed=['01','04','09','16','25','36','49','64','81'];
        return needed.every(sq=>{
            const d1=sq[0],d2=sq[1];
            return(a.includes(d1)&&b.includes(d2))||(a.includes(d2)&&b.includes(d1));
        });
    }
    let count=0;
    function dfs(cube1,cube2,pos) {
        if(cube1.length===6&&cube2.length===6) { if(valid(cube1,cube2)) count++; return; }
        if(pos>9) return;
        if(cube1.length<6) dfs(cube1+pos,cube2,pos+1);
        if(cube2.length<6) dfs(cube1,cube2+pos,pos+1);
        dfs(cube1,cube2,pos+1);
    }
    dfs('','',0);
    return count/2;
}

// 91: Right Triangles with Integer Coordinates
function rightTriangles(limit) {
    let count=0;
    for(let x1=0;x1<=limit;x1++) for(let y1=0;y1<=limit;y1++) {
        for(let x2=0;x2<=limit;x2++) for(let y2=0;y2<=limit;y2++) {
            if((x1===0&&y1===0)||(x2===0&&y2===0)||(x1===x2&&y1===y2)) continue;
            const a=x1*x1+y1*y1,b=x2*x2+y2*y2,c=(x2-x1)**2+(y2-y1)**2;
            if(a+b===c||a+c===b||b+c===a) count++;
        }
    }
    return count/2;
}

// 94: Almost Equilateral Triangles
function almostEquilateral(limit) {
    let sum=0;
    for(let n=1;6*n+2<=limit;n++) {
        const area2=Math.sqrt(3*n*n+4);
        if(area2===Math.floor(area2)) sum+=6*n+2;
    }
    for(let n=1;6*n-2<=limit;n++) {
        const area2=Math.sqrt(3*n*n-4);
        if(area2===Math.floor(area2)) sum+=6*n-2;
    }
    return sum;
}

// 95: Amicable Chains
function amicableChains(limit) {
    function d(n){let s=1;for(let i=2;i*i<=n;i++)if(n%i===0)s+=i+(i!==n/i?n/i:0);return s;}
    const minLink=new Array(limit+1).fill(0);
    let best=0,bestLen=0;
    for(let i=2;i<=limit;i++) {
        if(minLink[i]) continue;
        const chain=[i];let curr=i;
        while(true) { curr=d(curr); if(curr>limit||curr<=0) break; if(chain.includes(curr)) { const start=chain.indexOf(curr); const len=chain.length-start; if(len>bestLen) { bestLen=len; best=Math.min(...chain.slice(start)); } break; } chain.push(curr); }
    }
    return best;
}

// 96: Su Doku
function suDoku(grid) {
    function valid(grid,row,col,num) {
        for(let i=0;i<9;i++) if(grid[row][i]===num||grid[i][col]===num) return false;
        const br=Math.floor(row/3)*3,bc=Math.floor(col/3)*3;
        for(let i=br;imax) { max=logVal; best=i+1; }
    }
    return best;
}

// 100: Arranged Probability
function arrangedProbability() {
    let b=15,tt=21;
    while(tt<=1e12) { const nb=3*b+2*tt-2; const ntt=4*b+3*tt-3; b=nb; tt=ntt; }
    return b;
}
🌳 BASIC TREE DATA STRUCTURES
Fundamental tree structures for efficient data organization
Binary Tree, BST, AVL & Red-Black Tree
Core tree structures: basic binary, ordered BST, self-balancing AVL and Red-Black.
// Binary Tree Node
class TreeNode {
    constructor(val) { this.val=val; this.left=null; this.right=null; }
}

// Binary Search Tree
class BST {
    constructor() { this.root=null; }
    insert(val) { this.root=this._insert(this.root,val); }
    _insert(node,val) {
        if(!node) return new TreeNode(val);
        if(valnode.val) node.right=this._insert(node.right,val);
        return node;
    }
    search(val) { return this._search(this.root,val); }
    _search(node,val) {
        if(!node||node.val===val) return node;
        return valnode.val) node.right=this._delete(node.right,val);
        else {
            if(!node.left) return node.right;
            if(!node.right) return node.left;
            let succ=node.right; while(succ.left) succ=succ.left;
            node.val=succ.val; node.right=this._delete(node.right,succ.val);
        }
        return node;
    }
}

// AVL Tree
class AVLTree extends BST {
    constructor() { super(); }
    _insert(node,val) {
        node=super._insert(node,val);
        node=this._balance(node);
        return node;
    }
    _balance(node) {
        const bf=this._height(node.left)-this._height(node.right);
        if(bf>1) { if(this._height(node.left.left)>=this._height(node.left.right)) node=this._rotateRight(node); else { node.left=this._rotateLeft(node.left); node=this._rotateRight(node); } }
        if(bf<-1) { if(this._height(node.right.right)>=this._height(node.right.left)) node=this._rotateLeft(node); else { node.right=this._rotateRight(node.right); node=this._rotateLeft(node); } }
        return node;
    }
    _rotateRight(y) { const x=y.left; y.left=x.right; x.right=y; return x; }
    _rotateLeft(x) { const y=x.right; x.right=y.left; y.left=x; return y; }
    _height(node) { return node?1+Math.max(this._height(node.left),this._height(node.right)):0; }
}

Splay Tree, B-Tree, B+ Tree & Trie
Self-adjusting trees, disk-optimized trees, and prefix trees for strings.
// Splay Tree
class SplayTree {
    constructor() { this.root=null; }
    _splay(node, val) {
        if (!node || node.val === val) return node;
        if (val < node.val) {
            if (!node.left) return node;
            if (val < node.left.val) {
                node.left.left = this._splay(node.left.left, val);
                node = this._rotateRight(node);
            } else if (val > node.left.val) {
                node.left.right = this._splay(node.left.right, val);
                if (node.left.right) node.left = this._rotateLeft(node.left);
            }
            return node.left ? this._rotateRight(node) : node;
        } else {
            if (!node.right) return node;
            if (val > node.right.val) {
                node.right.right = this._splay(node.right.right, val);
                node = this._rotateLeft(node);
            } else if (val < node.right.val) {
                node.right.left = this._splay(node.right.left, val);
                if (node.right.left) node.right = this._rotateRight(node.right);
            }
            return node.right ? this._rotateLeft(node) : node;
        }
    }
    _rotateRight(x) { const y=x.left; x.left=y.right; y.right=x; return y; }
    _rotateLeft(x) { const y=x.right; x.right=y.left; y.left=x; return y; }
    insert(val) { this.root=this._splay(this.root,val); }
    search(val) { this.root=this._splay(this.root,val); return this.root&&this.root.val===val; }
}

// B-Tree (order 3)
class BTree {
    constructor(order) { this.order=order; this.root=new BTreeNode(true); }
    insert(key) { const root=this.root; if(root.keys.length===2*this.order-1) { const s=new BTreeNode(false); s.children[0]=root; this._splitChild(s,0); this.root=s; } this._insertNonFull(this.root,key); }
    _insertNonFull(node,key) { let i=node.keys.length-1; if(node.leaf) { node.keys.push(0); while(i>=0&&key=0&&keynode.keys[i]) i++; } this._insertNonFull(node.children[i],key); } }
    _splitChild(parent,i) { const order=this.order; const full=parent.children[i]; const node=new BTreeNode(full.leaf); parent.children.splice(i+1,0,node); parent.keys.splice(i,0,full.keys[order-1]); node.keys=full.keys.splice(order); if(!full.leaf) node.children=full.children.splice(order); }
}

class BTreeNode { constructor(leaf) { this.keys=[]; this.children=[]; this.leaf=leaf; } }

// Trie (Prefix Tree)
class Trie {
    constructor() { this.root={children:{},isEnd:false}; }
    insert(word) { let node=this.root; for(const ch of word) { if(!node.children[ch]) node.children[ch]={children:{},isEnd:false}; node=node.children[ch]; } node.isEnd=true; }
    search(word) { let node=this.root; for(const ch of word) { if(!node.children[ch]) return false; node=node.children[ch]; } return node.isEnd; }
    startsWith(prefix) { let node=this.root; for(const ch of prefix) { if(!node.children[ch]) return false; node=node.children[ch]; } return true; }
    getAllWords(node=this.root,prefix='') { const words=[]; if(node.isEnd) words.push(prefix); for(const[ch,child] of Object.entries(node.children)) words.push(...this.getAllWords(child,prefix+ch)); return words; }
}
Segment Tree, Fenwick Tree, Quadtree & Octree
Interval trees, prefix sums, and spatial partitioning trees.
// Segment Tree
class SegmentTree {
    constructor(data) {
        this.n=data.length;
        this.tree=new Array(4*this.n).fill(0);
        this._build(1,0,this.n-1,data);
    }
    _build(node,start,end,data) {
        if(start===end) { this.tree[node]=data[start]; return; }
        const mid=(start+end)>>1;
        this._build(2*node,start,mid,data);
        this._build(2*node+1,mid+1,end,data);
        this.tree[node]=this.tree[2*node]+this.tree[2*node+1];
    }
    query(l,r) { return this._query(1,0,this.n-1,l,r); }
    _query(node,start,end,l,r) {
        if(r>1;
        return this._query(2*node,start,mid,l,r)+this._query(2*node+1,mid+1,end,l,r);
    }
    update(idx,val) { this._update(1,0,this.n-1,idx,val); }
    _update(node,start,end,idx,val) {
        if(start===end) { this.tree[node]=val; return; }
        const mid=(start+end)>>1;
        if(idx<=mid) this._update(2*node,start,mid,idx,val);
        else this._update(2*node+1,mid+1,end,idx,val);
        this.tree[node]=this.tree[2*node]+this.tree[2*node+1];
    }
}

// Fenwick Tree (Binary Indexed Tree)
class FenwickTree {
    constructor(n) { this.n=n; this.tree=new Array(n+1).fill(0); }
    update(i,delta) { for(;i<=this.n;i+=i&(-i)) this.tree[i]+=delta; }
    query(i) { let sum=0; for(;i>0;i-=i&(-i)) sum+=this.tree[i]; return sum; }
    rangeQuery(l,r) { return this.query(r)-this.query(l-1); }
}

// Quadtree
class Quadtree {
    constructor(boundary,capacity) { this.boundary=boundary; this.capacity=capacity; this.points=[]; this.divided=false; }
    insert(point) {
        if(!this.contains(point)) return false;
        if(this.points.length=this.boundary.x&&point.x<=this.boundary.x+this.boundary.w&&point.y>=this.boundary.y&&point.y<=this.boundary.y+this.boundary.h; }
    subdivide() {
        const{x,y,w,h}=this.boundary;
        this.nw=new Quadtree({x,y,w:w/2,h:h/2},this.capacity);
        this.ne=new Quadtree({x:x+w/2,y,w:w/2,h:h/2},this.capacity);
        this.sw=new Quadtree({x,y:y+h/2,w:w/2,h:h/2},this.capacity);
        this.se=new Quadtree({x:x+w/2,y:y+h/2,w:w/2,h:h/2},this.capacity);
        this.divided=true;
    }
    query(range) {
        const found=[];
        if(!this.intersects(range)) return found;
        for(const p of this.points) if(this.inRange(p,range)) found.push(p);
        if(this.divided) found.push(...this.nw.query(range),...this.ne.query(range),...this.sw.query(range),...this.se.query(range));
        return found;
    }
    intersects(range) { return !(range.x>this.boundary.x+this.boundary.w||range.x+range.wthis.boundary.y+this.boundary.h||range.y+range.h=r.x&&p.x<=r.x+r.w&&p.y>=r.y&&p.y<=r.y+r.h; }
}

        if(this.points.length
Tournament, Decision, Treap, VP-Tree & BK-Tree
Tournament brackets, decision trees, randomized trees, and metric trees.
// Tournament Tree
class TournamentTree {
    constructor(data) { this.tree=[]; this.build(data); }
    build(data) {
        this.tree=[...data];
        let level=data.length;
        while(level>1) {
            for(let i=0;i5||X.length<2) return {leaf:true,prediction:Math.round(y.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/y.length)};
        let bestFeat=0,bestThresh=0,bestScore=Infinity;
        for(let f=0;fx[f]).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
            for(let t=0;t{if(x[f]<=thresh)leftIdx.push(i);else rightIdx.push(i);});
                if(leftIdx.length===0||rightIdx.length===0) continue;
                const score=this._gini(y,leftIdx)+this._gini(y,rightIdx);
                if(score{if(x[bestFeat]<=bestThresh)leftIdx.push(i);else rightIdx.push(i);});
        return {leaf:false,feature:bestFeat,threshold:bestThresh,left:this._build(leftIdx.map(i=>X[i]),leftIdx.map(i=>y[i]),depth+1),right:this._build(rightIdx.map(i=>X[i]),rightIdx.map(i=>y[i]),depth+1)};
    }
    _gini(y,indices) { if(indices.length===0) return 0; const counts={}; indices.forEach(i=>{counts[y[i]]=(counts[y[i]]||0)+1;}); return 1-Object.values(counts).reduce((s,c)=>s+(c/indices.length)**2,0); }
    predict(x,node=this.root) { if(node.leaf) return node.prediction; return x[node.feature]<=node.threshold?this.predict(x,node.left):this.predict(x,node.right); }
}

// Treap
class Treap {
    constructor() { this.root=null; }
    insert(val) { this.root=this._insert(this.root,val); }
    _insert(node,val) {
        if(!node) return {val,priority:Math.random(),left:null,right:null};
        if(valnode.priority) node=this._rotateRight(node); }
        else if(val>node.val) { node.right=this._insert(node.right,val); if(node.right.priority>node.priority) node=this._rotateLeft(node); }
        return node;
    }
    _rotateRight(y) { const x=y.left; y.left=x.right; x.right=y; return x; }
    _rotateLeft(x) { const y=x.right; x.right=y.left; y.left=x; return y; }
    inorder() { const r=[]; this._inorder(this.root,r); return r; }
    _inorder(node,r) { if(node){this._inorder(node.left,r);r.push(node.val);this._inorder(node.right,r);} }
}

// Vantage-Point Tree
class VPTree {
    constructor(points) { this.root=this._build(points); }
    _build(points) {
        if(points.length===0) return null;
        const idx=Math.floor(Math.random()*points.length);
        const vp=points[idx];
        const dists=points.map((p,i)=>({point:p,dist:this._dist(vp,p),idx:i})).filter(d=>d.idx!==idx).sort((a,b)=>a.dist-b.dist);
        const mid=Math.floor(dists.length/2);
        const median=dists.length>0?dists[mid].dist:0;
        return {vp,median,left:this._build(dists.slice(0,mid).map(d=>d.point)),right:this._build(dists.slice(mid).map(d=>d.point))};
    }
    _dist(a,b) { return Math.sqrt((a.x-b.x)**2+(a.y-b.y)**2); }
    nn(point) { let best={point:null,dist:Infinity}; this._nn(this.root,point,best); return best; }
    _nn(node,point,best) {
        if(!node) return;
        const d=this._dist(point,node.vp);
        if(d=best.dist) this._nn(node.right,point,best);
    }
}

// BK-Tree
class BKTree {
    constructor(distance) { this.root=null; this.distance=distance; }
    insert(word) { if(!this.root) { this.root={word,children:{}}; return; } this._insert(this.root,word); }
    _insert(node,word) { const d=this.distance(word,node.word); if(d===0) return; if(!node.children[d]) node.children[d]={word,children:{}}; else this._insert(node.children[d],word); }
    search(word,maxDist) { return this._search(this.root,word,maxDist); }
    _search(node,word,maxDist) {
        if(!node) return [];
        const d=this.distance(word,node.word);
        const results=[];
        if(d<=maxDist) results.push({word:node.word,dist:d});
        for(let i=Math.max(0,d-maxDist);i<=d+maxDist;i++) if(node.children[i]) results.push(...this._search(node.children[i],word,maxDist));
        return results;
    }
}
🔢 50 NUMBER TYPES & THEORIES
Special number properties and mathematical classifications
Number Types #1-12
Spy, Neon, Evil, Armstrong, Perfect, Happy, Sad, Harshad, Abundant, Deficient, Amicable, Automorphic.
// 1: Spy Number (sum of digits == product)
function isSpy(n) {
    const digits = n.toString().split('').map(Number);
    const sum = digits.reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
    const prod = digits.reduce((a,b) => a*b, 1);
    return sum === prod;
}

// 2: Neon Number (sum of digits of square == n)
function isNeon(n) {
    const sq = (n*n).toString().split('').reduce((s,d) => s+parseInt(d), 0);
    return sq === n;
}

// 3: Evil Number (even number of 1s in binary)
function isEvil(n) {
    const ones = n.toString(2).split('').filter(c => c==='1').length;
    return ones % 2 === 0;
}

// 4: Armstrong Number (narcissistic)
function isArmstrong(n) {
    const digits = n.toString().split('').map(Number);
    const power = digits.length;
    return digits.reduce((s,d) => s + Math.pow(d,power), 0) === n;
}

// 5: Perfect Number (sum of proper divisors == n)
function isPerfect(n) {
    let sum = 1;
    for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) { if (n%i===0) { sum += i; if (i !== n/i) sum += n/i; } }
    return sum === n && n !== 1;
}

// 6: Happy Number (reaches 1 with sum of squares of digits)
function isHappy(n) {
    const seen = new Set();
    while (n !== 1 && !seen.has(n)) { seen.add(n); n = n.toString().split('').reduce((s,d) => s+d*d, 0); }
    return n === 1;
}

// 7: Sad Number (never reaches 1)
function isSad(n) { return !isHappy(n); }

// 8: Harshad Number (divisible by sum of digits)
function isHarshad(n) {
    const sum = n.toString().split('').reduce((s,d) => s+parseInt(d), 0);
    return n % sum === 0;
}

// 9: Abundant Number (sum of divisors > n)
function isAbundant(n) {
    let sum = 1;
    for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) { if (n%i===0) { sum += i; if (i !== n/i) sum += n/i; } }
    return sum > n;
}

// 10: Deficient Number (sum of divisors < n)
function isDeficient(n) {
    let sum = 1;
    for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) { if (n%i===0) { sum += i; if (i !== n/i) sum += n/i; } }
    return sum < n;
}

// 11: Amicable Numbers
function isAmicable(n) {
    function d(num) { let s=1; for(let i=2;i*i<=num;i++) if(num%i===0) s+=i+(i!==num/i?num/i:0); return s; }
    const m = d(n);
    return m !== n && d(m) === n;
}

// 12: Automorphic Number (square ends with n)
function isAutomorphic(n) {
    const sq = (n*n).toString();
    return sq.endsWith(n.toString());
}
Number Types #13-25
Fibonacci, Lucas, Palindromic, Prime, Twin Primes, Mersenne, Square, Cubic, Triangular, Smith, Kaprekar, Disarium.
// 13: Fibonacci Number
function isFibonacci(n) {
    function isSq(x) { const s=Math.round(Math.sqrt(x)); return s*s===x; }
    return isSq(5*n*n+4) || isSq(5*n*n-4);
}

// 14: Lucas Number
function isLucas(n) {
    function isSq(x) { const s=Math.round(Math.sqrt(x)); return s*s===x; }
    return isSq(5*n*n+20) || isSq(5*n*n-20);
}

// 15: Palindromic Number
function isPalindromic(n) {
    const s = n.toString();
    return s === s.split('').reverse().join('');
}

// 16: Prime Number
function isPrime(n) {
    if (n < 2) return false;
    for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) if (n%i===0) return false;
    return true;
}

// 17: Composite Number
function isComposite(n) { return n > 1 && !isPrime(n); }

// 18: Twin Primes
function isTwinPrime(n) { return isPrime(n) && (isPrime(n-2) || isPrime(n+2)); }

// 19: Mersenne Prime (2^p - 1)
function isMersenne(n) {
    for (let p = 2; p <= 31; p++) {
        const m = Math.pow(2,p) - 1;
        if (m === n && isPrime(p)) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// 20: Square Number
function isSquare(n) { const s=Math.round(Math.sqrt(n)); return s*s===n; }

// 21: Cubic Number
function isCubic(n) { const s=Math.round(Math.cbrt(n)); return s*s*s===n; }

// 22: Triangular Number
function isTriangular(n) {
    const x = Math.sqrt(8*n+1);
    return x === Math.floor(x);
}

// 23: Smith Number
function smithNumber(n) {
    function digitSum(num) { return num.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s+parseInt(d),0); }
    function primeFactors(num) {
        const factors = [];
        for (let i = 2; i*i <= num; i++) { while(num%i===0) { factors.push(i); num/=i; } }
        if (num > 1) factors.push(num);
        return factors;
    }
    return isComposite(n) && digitSum(n) === primeFactors(n).reduce((s,f)=>s+digitSum(f),0);
}

// 24: Kaprekar Number
function isKaprekar(n) {
    const sq = (n*n).toString();
    for (let i = 1; i < sq.length; i++) {
        const left = parseInt(sq.substring(0,i)) || 0;
        const right = parseInt(sq.substring(i)) || 0;
        if (left + right === n && right !== 0) return true;
    }
    return n === 1;
}

// 25: Disarium Number
function isDisarium(n) {
    const digits = n.toString().split('').map(Number);
    return digits.reduce((s,d,i) => s + Math.pow(d,i+1), 0) === n;
}
Number Types #26-38
Pronic, Catalan, Factorial, Strong, Magic, Collatz, Ulam, Semi-Prime, Taxicab, Keith, Odish, Evenish, Duck.
// 26: Pronic Number (n * (n+1))
function isPronic(n) {
    for (let i = 0; i*i <= n; i++) { if (i*(i+1) === n) return true; }
    return false;
}

// 27: Catalan Number
function catalan(n) {
    if (n <= 1) return 1;
    let res = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) res += catalan(i) * catalan(n-i-1);
    return res;
}

// 28: Factorial Number
function factorial(n) { return n <= 1 ? 1 : n * factorial(n-1); }
function isFactorial(n) { for (let i=1; ; i++) { const f=factorial(i); if(f===n)return true; if(f>n)return false; } }

// 29: Strong Number (sum of digit factorials == n)
function isStrong(n) {
    const facts=[1,1,2,6,24,120,720,5040,40320,362880];
    return n.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s+facts[parseInt(d)],0) === n;
}

// 30: Magic Number (digital root == 1)
function isMagic(n) { while(n>=10) n=n.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s+parseInt(d),0); return n===1; }

// 31: Collatz Sequence
function collatz(n) { const seq=[n]; while(n!==1) { n=n%2===0?n/2:3*n+1; seq.push(n); } return seq; }

// 32: Ulam Number
function ulamNumbers(count) {
    const seq=[1,2];
    for (let n=3; seq.length1) factors.push(num);
    return factors.length===2 && factors[0]!==factors[1] || (factors.length===3 && factors[0]===factors[1] && factors[2]!==factors[0]);
}

// 34: Taxicab Number
function taxicab(n) {
    const ways=new Set();
    for (let i=1; i<=n*100; i++) for (let j=i; j<=n*100; j++) {
        const sum=i*i*i+j*j*j;
        if (ways.has(sum)) return sum;
        ways.add(sum);
    }
    return 1729;
}

// 35: Keith Number
function isKeith(n) {
    const digits=n.toString().split('').map(Number);
    const seq=[...digits];
    while (seq[seq.length-1] < n) {
        const next=seq.slice(-digits.length).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
        seq.push(next);
        if (next===n) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// 36: Odish Number (sum of digits is odd)
function isOdish(n) { return n.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s+parseInt(d),0)%2===1; }

// 37: Evenish Number (sum of digits is even)
function isEvenish(n) { return n.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s+parseInt(d),0)%2===0; }

// 38: Duck Number (contains zero, not at start)
function isDuck(n) { const s=n.toString(); return s.length>1 && s.slice(1).includes('0'); }
Number Types #39-50
Cyclic, Friedman, Goldbach, Circular Prime, Smarandache, Untouchable, Practical, Symmetric, Woodall, Cullen, Sphenic.
// 39: Cyclic Number
function isCyclic(n) {
    const s=n.toString();
    for (let i=1; i<=s.length; i++) {
        const product=(n*i).toString();
        if (product.length===s.length && new Set(product.split('')).size===new Set(s.split('')).size) continue;
        else if (product.length===s.length) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// 40: Friedman Number
function isFriedman(n) {
    const digits=n.toString().split('').map(Number);
    for (let a=1; a<=9999; a++) for (let b=1; b<=9999; b++) {
        if (a*b===n) {
            const used=(a.toString()+b.toString()).split('').sort().join('');
            if (used===digits.sort().join('')) return true;
        }
        if (a+b===n) {
            const used=(a.toString()+b.toString()).split('').sort().join('');
            if (used===digits.sort().join('')) return true;
        }
    }
    return n===25; // 5^2
}

// 41: Goldbach's Conjecture
function goldbach(n) {
    if (n%2!==0||n<=2) return null;
    function isPrime(x) { if(x<2)return false;for(let i=2;i*i<=x;i++)if(x%i===0)return false;return true; }
    for (let i=2; i<=n/2; i++) { if (isPrime(i)&&isPrime(n-i)) return [i,n-i]; }
    return null;
}

// 42: Circular Prime
function isCircularPrime(n) {
    function isPrime(x) { if(x<2)return false;for(let i=2;i*i<=x;i++)if(x%i===0)return false;return true; }
    const s=n.toString();
    for (let i=0; isum+1) return false; sum+=i; } }
    return sum>=n;
}

// 46: Symmetric Number (palindrome)
function isSymmetric(n) { const s=n.toString(); return s===s.split('').reverse().join(''); }

// 47: Abundant Power Number
function isAbundantPower(n) {
    function isPower(x) { for(let b=2;b*b<=x;b++){let a=Math.round(Math.pow(x,1/Math.log2(Math.log(x)/Math.log(b))));if(Math.pow(b,a)===x)return true;}return false; }
    let sum=1; for(let i=2;i*i<=n;i++)if(n%i===0){sum+=i;if(i!==n/i)sum+=n/i;}
    return sum>n && isPower(n);
}

// 48: Woodall Number (n * 2^n - 1)
function isWoodall(n) { for(let i=1;i<=20;i++){if(i*Math.pow(2,i)-1===n)return true;}return false; }

// 49: Cullen Number (n * 2^n + 1)
function isCullen(n) { for(let i=1;i<=20;i++){if(i*Math.pow(2,i)+1===n)return true;}return false; }

// 50: Sphenic Number (product of 3 distinct primes)
function isSphenic(n) {
    const factors=[];
    let num=n;
    for(let i=2;i*i<=num;i++) while(num%i===0){factors.push(i);num/=i;}
    if(num>1)factors.push(num);
    return factors.length===3 && new Set(factors).size===3;
}
🧮 ADVANCED NUMBER TYPES & THEORIES
Specialized mathematical number classifications and sequences
Number Types #1-15
Abundancy, Admirable, Amenable, Apocalyptic, Balancing, Belphegor, Bouncing, Blair, Cake, Carol, Centered Square, Concentric, Demlo, Digital Root, Emirp.
// 1: Abundancy Index
function abundancyIndex(n) {
    let sum = 1;
    for (let i = 2; i*i <= n; i++) { if (n%i===0) { sum += i; if (i!==n/i) sum += n/i; } }
    return sum / n;
}

// 2: Admirable Number
function isAdmirable(n) {
    const divisors = [1];
    for (let i = 2; i <= n/2; i++) { if (n%i===0) divisors.push(i); }
    for (let i = 0; i < divisors.length; i++) {
        const sum = divisors.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0) - 2*divisors[i];
        if (sum === n) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// 3: Amenable Number
function isAmenable(n) {
    if (n===4) return true;
    for (let a=1; a<=n; a++) for (let b=1; b<=n; b++) {
        if (a*b===n && a+b===n) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// 4: Apocalyptic Number
function isApocalyptic(n) {
    const pow = BigInt(2)**BigInt(n);
    return pow.toString().includes('666');
}

// 5: Balancing Number
function isBalancing(n) {
    const leftSum = n*(n-1)/2;
    let rightSum = 0, m = n+1;
    while (rightSum < leftSum) { rightSum += m; m++; }
    return rightSum === leftSum;
}

// 6: Belphegor's Prime
function isBelphegor(n) {
    return n.toString() === '1000000000000066600000000000001';
}

// 7: Bouncing Number
function isBouncing(n) {
    const s = n.toString();
    let up = false, down = false;
    for (let i = 1; i < s.length; i++) {
        if (s[i] > s[i-1]) up = true;
        if (s[i] < s[i-1]) down = true;
    }
    return up && down;
}

// 8: Cake Number
function cakeNumber(n) { return (n*n*n + 5*n + 6) / 6; }

// 9: Carol Number
function carolNumber(n) { return Math.pow(Math.pow(2,n)-1, 2) - 2; }

// 10: Centered Square Number
function centeredSquare(n) { return 2*n*n - 2*n + 1; }

// 11: Concentric Number
function isConcentric(n) { const s=Math.sqrt(n); return s===Math.floor(s) && s%2===1; }

// 12: Demlo Number
function isDemlo(n) { const s=Math.round(Math.sqrt(n)); return s*s===n && /^1+$/.test(s.toString()); }

// 13: Digital Root
function digitalRoot(n) { while(n>=10) n=n.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s+parseInt(d),0); return n; }

// 14: Emirp (reverse is different prime)
function isEmirp(n) {
    function isPrime(x) { if(x<2)return false;for(let i=2;i*i<=x;i++)if(x%i===0)return false;return true; }
    const rev = parseInt(n.toString().split('').reverse().join(''));
    return isPrime(n) && isPrime(rev) && n !== rev;
}

// 15: Equidigital Number
function isEquidigital(n) {
    const nDigits = n.toString().length;
    let factors = '', num = n;
    for (let i = 2; i*i <= num; i++) { while(num%i===0) { factors += i.toString(); num /= i; } }
    if (num > 1) factors += num.toString();
    return nDigits === factors.length;
}

function fortunateNumber(n) {

// 19: Giuga Number
function isGiuga(n) {
    function isPrime(x){if(x<2)return false;for(let i=2;i*i<=x;i++)if(x%i===0)return false;return true;}
    const factors=[];let num=n;
    for(let i=2;i*i<=num;i++){while(num%i===0){factors.push(i);num/=i;}}
    if(num>1)factors.push(num);
    return factors.every(p=>((n/p)-1)%p===0);
}

// 20: Highly Composite Number
function isHighlyComposite(n) {
    function countDivisors(x){let c=0;for(let i=1;i*i<=x;i++)if(x%i===0)c+=i*i===x?1:2;return c;}
    const nDivs = countDivisors(n);
    for(let i=1;i=nDivs) return false;
    return true;
}

// 21: Interprime
function isInterprime(n) {
    function isPrime(x){if(x<2)return false;for(let i=2;i*i<=x;i++)if(x%i===0)return false;return true;}
    let prev=0, next=0;
    for(let i=n-1;i>=2;i--)if(isPrime(i)){prev=i;break;}
    for(let i=n+1;;i++)if(isPrime(i)){next=i;break;}
    return prev+next===2*n;
}

// 22: Kynea Number
function kyneaNumber(n) { return Math.pow(Math.pow(2,n)+1, 2) - 2; }

// 23: Lychrel Number (simplified - check if reaches palindrome in 50 steps)
function isLychrel(n) {
    let x = BigInt(n);
    for(let i=0;i<50;i++){
        const rev = BigInt(x.toString().split('').reverse().join(''));
        x = x + rev;
        if(x.toString()===x.toString().split('').reverse().join('')) return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// 24: Munchausen Number
function isMunchausen(n) {
    return n.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>{const x=parseInt(d);return s+(x===0?0:Math.pow(x,x));},0)===n;
}

// 25: Padovan Number
function padovan(n) {
    const p=[1,1,1];
    for(let i=3;i<=n;i++) p[i]=p[i-2]+p[i-3];

}

    const p=[0,1];

    return p[n];
}

function perrinNumber(n) {
    const p=[3,0,2];
    for(let i=3;i<=n;i++) p[i]=p[i-2]+p[i-3];
    return p[n];
}

    return true;
}

// 31: Rhonda Number
function isRhonda(n) {
    const digitProd = n.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s*parseInt(d),1);
    const factors=[];let num=n;
    for(let i=2;i*i<=num;i++){while(num%i===0){factors.push(i);num/=i;}}
    if(num>1)factors.push(num);
    const factorSum = factors.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
    return digitProd % factorSum === 0;
}

// 32: Ruth-Aaron Pair
function isRuthAaron(a, b) {
    function primeFactorSum(x) {
        const factors=[];let num=x;
        for(let i=2;i*i<=num;i++){while(num%i===0){factors.push(i);num/=i;}}
        if(num>1)factors.push(num);
        return factors.reduce((s,f)=>s+f,0);
    }
    return primeFactorSum(a) === primeFactorSum(b);
}

// 33: Self Number
function isSelfNumber(n) {
    function gen(x) { return x + x.toString().split('').reduce((s,d)=>s+parseInt(d),0); }
    for(let i=1;ia+b,0)<=n) return false;

    for(const d of divisors) for(let j=n;j>=d;j--) if(dp[j-d])dp[j]=true;
    return !dp[n];
}

// 39: Polydivisible Number
function isPolydivisible(n) {

    for(let i=1;i<=s.length;i++){
        if(parseInt(s.substring(0,i))%i!==0) return false;
    }
    return true;
}
