Pascal's Triangle
Binomial coefficients, combinations, and row-based factor calculations.
// Generate Pascal's Triangle
function pascalTriangle(n) {
    const triangle = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        triangle[i] = [1];
        for (let j = 1; j < i; j++) {
            triangle[i][j] = triangle[i-1][j-1] + triangle[i-1][j];
        }
        if (i > 0) triangle[i].push(1);
    }
    return triangle;
}

// C(n, k) - binomial coefficient
function C(n, k) {
    if (k > n) return 0;
    if (k === 0 || k === n) return 1;
    let res = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < k; i++) {
        res = res * (n - i) / (i + 1);
    }
    return Math.round(res);
}

// Factorial
function factorial(n) {
    return n <= 1 ? 1 : n * factorial(n - 1);
}

// Factorial factors (prime factorization)
function factorialFactors(n) {
    const factors = {};
    for (let i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
        let x = i;
        for (let p = 2; p * p <= x; p++) {
            while (x % p === 0) {
                factors[p] = (factors[p] || 0) + 1;
                x /= p;
            }
        }
        if (x > 1) factors[x] = (factors[x] || 0) + 1;
    }
    return factors;
}

// Catalan number from Pascal
function catalanFromPascal(n) {
    return C(2*n, n) / (n + 1);
}

// Row sum
function pascalRowSum(n) {
    return Math.pow(2, n);
}

// Odd numbers in row
function pascalOddCount(n) {
    let count = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k <= n; k++) {
        if (C(n, k) % 2 === 1) count++;
    }
    return count;
}

// Print Pascal's Triangle
function printPascal(n) {
    const triangle = pascalTriangle(n);
    return triangle.map(row => row.join(' ')).join('
');
}

// Pascal's Triangle properties
function pascalProperties(n) {
    return {
        row: n,
        entries: pascalTriangle(n+1)[n],
        sum: pascalRowSum(n),
        oddCount: pascalOddCount(n),
        maxVal: Math.max(...pascalTriangle(n+1)[n])
    };
}
Leibniz Triangle
Harmonic triangle of fractions with reciprocal relationships.
// Generate Leibniz Triangle
function leibnizTriangle(n) {
    const triangle = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        triangle[i] = [];
        for (let j = 0; j <= i; j++) {
            if (j === 0 || j === i) {
                triangle[i][j] = 1 / (i + 1);
            } else {
                // Leibniz: L(i,j) = L(i-1,j-1) - L(i,j-1)
                triangle[i][j] = triangle[i-1][j-1] - triangle[i][j-1];
            }
        }
    }
    return triangle;
}

// Leibniz harmonic series sum
function leibnizHarmonic(n) {
    return n === 1 ? 1 : 1 / (n * (n + 1));
}

// Leibniz element at position (n, k)
function leibnizElement(n, k) {
    return 1 / ((n + 1) * C(n, k));
}

// Leibniz row sum
function leibnizRowSum(n) {
    return 1 / (n + 1);
}

// Leibniz diagonal sum (harmonic series approximation)
function leibnizDiagonalSum(n) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        sum += 1 / i;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Compare Pascal vs Leibniz
function pascalVsLeibniz(n) {
    const pascal = pascalTriangle(n);
    const leibniz = leibnizTriangle(n);
    return {
        pascalRow: pascal[n-1],
        leibnizRow: leibniz[n-1],
        pascalSum: pascalRowSum(n-1),
        leibnizSum: leibnizRowSum(n-1)
    };
}

// Leibniz infinite series (pi/4)
function leibnizPi(iterations) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < iterations; i++) {
        sum += Math.pow(-1, i) / (2 * i + 1);
    }
    return sum * 4;
}

// Print Leibniz Triangle
function printLeibniz(n) {
    const triangle = leibnizTriangle(n);
    return triangle.map(row => row.map(x => x.toFixed(6)).join(' ')).join('
');
}

// Leibniz Triangle properties
function leibnizProperties(n) {
    return {
        row: n,
        entries: leibnizTriangle(n+1)[n],
        sum: leibnizRowSum(n),
        firstElement: 1/(n+1),
        lastElement: 1/(n+1),
        piApprox: leibnizPi(1000)
    };
}

// Helper functions
function C(n, k) {
    if (k > n) return 0;
    if (k === 0 || k === n) return 1;
    let res = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < k; i++) res = res * (n - i) / (i + 1);
    return Math.round(res);
}
function pascalRowSum(n) { return Math.pow(2, n); }
function pascalTriangle(n) {
    const t = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        t[i] = [1];
        for (let j = 1; j < i; j++) t[i][j] = t[i-1][j-1] + t[i-1][j];
        if (i > 0) t[i].push(1);
    }
    return t;
}
🧩 DYNAMIC PROGRAMMING PATTERNS
Classic DP problems with Fibonacci, Knapsack, and 2D Grid patterns
Climbing Stairs / Unique Paths
Fibonacci-style DP. dp[i] = dp[i-1] + dp[i-2]. Optimize space to O(1) variables.
// Climbing Stairs - O(n) time, O(1) space
function climbStairs(n) {
    if (n <= 2) return n;
    let prev1 = 2, prev2 = 1;
    for (let i = 3; i <= n; i++) {
        let curr = prev1 + prev2;
        prev2 = prev1;
        prev1 = curr;
    }
    return prev1;
}

// Unique Paths in Grid - O(m*n) time
function uniquePaths(m, n) {
    const dp = Array(m).fill(0).map(() => Array(n).fill(1));
    for (let i = 1; i < m; i++)
        for (let j = 1; j < n; j++)
            dp[i][j] = dp[i-1][j] + dp[i][j-1];
    return dp[m-1][n-1];
}

// Unique Paths with Obstacles
function uniquePathsWithObstacles(grid) {
    const m = grid.length, n = grid[0].length;
    if (grid[0][0] === 1 || grid[m-1][n-1] === 1) return 0;
    const dp = Array(m).fill(0).map(() => Array(n).fill(0));
    dp[0][0] = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < m; i++)
        for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
            if (grid[i][j] === 1) { dp[i][j] = 0; continue; }
            if (i > 0) dp[i][j] += dp[i-1][j];
            if (j > 0) dp[i][j] += dp[i][j-1];
        }
    return dp[m-1][n-1];
}

// Minimum Path Sum
function minPathSum(grid) {
    const m = grid.length, n = grid[0].length;
    const dp = Array(m).fill(0).map(() => Array(n).fill(0));
    dp[0][0] = grid[0][0];
    for (let i = 1; i < m; i++) dp[i][0] = dp[i-1][0] + grid[i][0];
    for (let j = 1; j < n; j++) dp[0][j] = dp[0][j-1] + grid[0][j];
    for (let i = 1; i < m; i++)
        for (let j = 1; j < n; j++)
            dp[i][j] = Math.min(dp[i-1][j], dp[i][j-1]) + grid[i][j];
    return dp[m-1][n-1];
}

// Climbing Stairs with Cost
function minCostClimbingStairs(cost) {
    const n = cost.length;
    let prev1 = cost[1], prev2 = cost[0];
    for (let i = 2; i < n; i++) {
        let curr = cost[i] + Math.min(prev1, prev2);
        prev2 = prev1;
        prev1 = curr;
    }
    return Math.min(prev1, prev2);
}
Coin Change / Combination Sum
Unbounded Knapsack pattern. 1D array where dp[i] updates using dp[i - coin].
// Coin Change - Minimum coins
function coinChange(coins, amount) {
    const dp = new Array(amount + 1).fill(Infinity);
    dp[0] = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i <= amount; i++)
        for (const coin of coins)
            if (coin <= i && dp[i - coin] + 1 < dp[i])
                dp[i] = dp[i - coin] + 1;
    return dp[amount] === Infinity ? -1 : dp[amount];
}

// Coin Change - Number of ways
function coinChangeWays(coins, amount) {
    const dp = new Array(amount + 1).fill(0);
    dp[0] = 1;
    for (const coin of coins)
        for (let i = coin; i <= amount; i++)
            dp[i] += dp[i - coin];
    return dp[amount];
}

// Combination Sum
function combinationSum(candidates, target) {
    const result = [];
    function backtrack(start, remaining, path) {
        if (remaining === 0) { result.push([...path]); return; }
        for (let i = start; i < candidates.length; i++) {
            if (candidates[i] > remaining) break;
            path.push(candidates[i]);
            backtrack(i, remaining - candidates[i], path);
            path.pop();
        }
    }
    candidates.sort((a,b) => a - b);
    backtrack(0, target, []);
    return result;
}

// Unbounded Knapsack
function unboundedKnapsack(W, wt, val) {
    const dp = new Array(W + 1).fill(0);
    for (let i = 0; i <= W; i++)
        for (let j = 0; j < wt.length; j++)
            if (wt[j] <= i)
                dp[i] = Math.max(dp[i], dp[i - wt[j]] + val[j]);
    return dp[W];
}

// Rod Cutting
function rodCutting(prices, n) {
    const dp = new Array(n + 1).fill(0);
    for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++)
        for (let j = 0; j < i; j++)
            dp[i] = Math.max(dp[i], prices[j] + dp[i - j - 1]);
    return dp[n];
}
House Robber (I & II)
dp[i] = max(dp[i-1], dp[i-2] + current). For II, run twice skipping first/last house.
// House Robber I - O(n) time, O(1) space
function rob(nums) {
    let prev1 = 0, prev2 = 0;
    for (const num of nums) {
        let temp = Math.max(prev1, prev2 + num);
        prev2 = prev1;
        prev1 = temp;
    }
    return prev1;
}

// House Robber II - Circular houses
function robCircular(nums) {
    if (nums.length === 1) return nums[0];
    return Math.max(
        robRange(nums, 0, nums.length - 2),
        robRange(nums, 1, nums.length - 1)
    );
}

function robRange(nums, start, end) {
    let prev1 = 0, prev2 = 0;
    for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {
        let temp = Math.max(prev1, prev2 + nums[i]);
        prev2 = prev1;
        prev1 = temp;
    }
    return prev1;
}

// House Robber III - Binary Tree
function robTree(root) {
    function dfs(node) {
        if (!node) return [0, 0];
        const left = dfs(node.left);
        const right = dfs(node.right);
        return [
            Math.max(left[0], left[1]) + Math.max(right[0], right[1]),
            node.val + left[0] + right[0]
        ];
    }
    return Math.max(...dfs(root));
}

// House Robber with DELETE option
function robWithDelete(nums) {
    let prev1 = 0, prev2 = 0;
    for (const num of nums) {
        let temp = Math.max(prev1, prev2 + num);
        prev2 = prev1;
        prev1 = temp;
    }
    return prev1;
}

// Two houses adjacent max
function twoAdjacentMax(nums) {
    let sum1 = 0, sum2 = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < nums.length; i++) {
        if (i % 2 === 0) sum1 += nums[i];
        else sum2 += nums[i];
    }
    return Math.max(sum1, sum2);
}
Longest Common Subsequence / Edit Distance
2D Grid DP. Compare string1[i] with string2[j] and match diagonal vs adjacent cells.
// Longest Common Subsequence
function lcs(s1, s2) {
    const m = s1.length, n = s2.length;
    const dp = Array(m+1).fill(0).map(() => Array(n+1).fill(0));
    for (let i = 1; i <= m; i++)
        for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++)
            dp[i][j] = s1[i-1] === s2[j-1]
                ? dp[i-1][j-1] + 1
                : Math.max(dp[i-1][j], dp[i][j-1]);
    return dp[m][n];
}

// Edit Distance (Levenshtein)
function editDistance(s1, s2) {
    const m = s1.length, n = s2.length;
    const dp = Array(m+1).fill(0).map(() => Array(n+1).fill(0));
    for (let i = 0; i <= m; i++) dp[i][0] = i;
    for (let j = 0; j <= n; j++) dp[0][j] = j;
    for (let i = 1; i <= m; i++)
        for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++)
            dp[i][j] = s1[i-1] === s2[j-1]
                ? dp[i-1][j-1]
                : 1 + Math.min(dp[i-1][j], dp[i][j-1], dp[i-1][j-1]);
    return dp[m][n];
}

// Longest Common Substring
function longestCommonSubstring(s1, s2) {
    const m = s1.length, n = s2.length;
    const dp = Array(m+1).fill(0).map(() => Array(n+1).fill(0));
    let maxLen = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i <= m; i++)
        for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++) {
            if (s1[i-1] === s2[j-1]) dp[i][j] = dp[i-1][j-1] + 1;
            maxLen = Math.max(maxLen, dp[i][j]);
        }
    return maxLen;
}

// LCS with path reconstruction
function lcsWithPath(s1, s2) {
    const m = s1.length, n = s2.length;
    const dp = Array(m+1).fill(0).map(() => Array(n+1).fill(0));
    for (let i = 1; i <= m; i++)
        for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++)
            dp[i][j] = s1[i-1] === s2[j-1]
                ? dp[i-1][j-1] + 1
                : Math.max(dp[i-1][j], dp[i][j-1]);
    let i = m, j = n, path = '';
    while (i > 0 && j > 0) {
        if (s1[i-1] === s2[j-1]) { path = s1[i-1] + path; i--; j--; }
        else if (dp[i-1][j] > dp[i][j-1]) i--;
        else j--;
    }
    return { length: dp[m][n], path };
}
Longest Palindromic Substring
Expand Around Center trick (O(1) space) or 2D interval DP.
// Expand Around Center - O(n^2) time, O(1) space
function longestPalindrome(s) {
    let start = 0, maxLen = 1;
    function expand(l, r) {
        while (l >= 0 && r < s.length && s[l] === s[r]) {
            if (r - l + 1 > maxLen) { start = l; maxLen = r - l + 1; }
            l--; r++;
        }
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
        expand(i, i);     // Odd length
        expand(i, i + 1); // Even length
    }
    return s.substring(start, start + maxLen);
}

// 2D DP Approach
function longestPalindromeDP(s) {
    const n = s.length;
    const dp = Array(n).fill(0).map(() => Array(n).fill(false));
    let start = 0, maxLen = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) dp[i][i] = true;
    for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
        if (s[i] === s[i+1]) { dp[i][i+1] = true; start = i; maxLen = 2; }
    }
    for (let len = 3; len <= n; len++)
        for (let i = 0; i <= n - len; i++) {
            const j = i + len - 1;
            if (s[i] === s[j] && dp[i+1][j-1]) {
                dp[i][j] = true;
                if (len > maxLen) { start = i; maxLen = len; }
            }
        }
    return s.substring(start, start + maxLen);
}

// Longest Palindromic Subsequence
function longestPalindromicSubseq(s) {
    const n = s.length;
    const dp = Array(n).fill(0).map(() => Array(n).fill(0));
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) dp[i][i] = 1;
    for (let len = 2; len <= n; len++)
        for (let i = 0; i <= n - len; i++) {
            const j = i + len - 1;
            if (s[i] === s[j]) dp[i][j] = dp[i+1][j-1] + 2;
            else dp[i][j] = Math.max(dp[i+1][j], dp[i][j-1]);
        }
    return dp[0][n-1];
}

// Count Palindromic Substrings
function countPalindromicSubstrings(s) {
    let count = 0;
    function expand(l, r) {
        while (l >= 0 && r < s.length && s[l] === s[r]) { count++; l--; r++; }
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) { expand(i, i); expand(i, i+1); }
    return count;
}
Longest Increasing Subsequence
O(N^2) DP or O(N log N) with Binary Search (Patience Sorting).
// O(N^2) DP
function lisDP(nums) {
    const n = nums.length;
    const dp = new Array(n).fill(1);
    let maxLen = 1;
    for (let i = 1; i < n; i++)
        for (let j = 0; j < i; j++)
            if (nums[j] < nums[i]) {
                dp[i] = Math.max(dp[i], dp[j] + 1);
                maxLen = Math.max(maxLen, dp[i]);
            }
    return maxLen;
}

// O(N log N) - Patience Sorting with Binary Search
function lisBinarySearch(nums) {
    const tails = [];
    for (const num of nums) {
        let lo = 0, hi = tails.length;
        while (lo < hi) {
            const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
            if (tails[mid] < num) lo = mid + 1;
            else hi = mid;
        }
        tails[lo] = num;
    }
    return tails.length;
}

// LIS with path reconstruction
function lisWithPath(nums) {
    const n = nums.length;
    const dp = new Array(n).fill(1);
    const prev = new Array(n).fill(-1);
    let maxLen = 1, endIdx = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < n; i++)
        for (let j = 0; j < i; j++)
            if (nums[j] < nums[i] && dp[j] + 1 > dp[i]) {
                dp[i] = dp[j] + 1;
                prev[i] = j;
                if (dp[i] > maxLen) { maxLen = dp[i]; endIdx = i; }
            }
    const path = [];
    let idx = endIdx;
    while (idx !== -1) { path.unshift(nums[idx]); idx = prev[idx]; }
    return { length: maxLen, path };
}

// Longest Decreasing Subsequence
function lds(nums) { return lisDP(nums.map(x => -x)); }

// Longest Bitonic Subsequence
function longestBitonic(nums) {
    const n = nums.length;
    const lis = new Array(n).fill(1), lds = new Array(n).fill(1);
    for (let i = 1; i < n; i++)
        for (let j = 0; j < i; j++)
            if (nums[j] < nums[i]) lis[i] = Math.max(lis[i], lis[j] + 1);
    for (let i = n-2; i >= 0; i--)
        for (let j = n-1; j > i; j--)
            if (nums[j] < nums[i]) lds[i] = Math.max(lds[i], lds[j] + 1);
    let max = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) max = Math.max(max, lis[i] + lds[i] - 1);
    return max;
}
Maximal Square
dp[i][j] = min(top, left, top_left) + 1 for matching cells.
// Maximal Square in Binary Matrix
function maximalSquare(matrix) {
    const m = matrix.length, n = matrix[0].length;
    const dp = Array(m+1).fill(0).map(() => Array(n+1).fill(0));
    let maxSide = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i <= m; i++)
        for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++)
            if (matrix[i-1][j-1] === '1') {
                dp[i][j] = Math.min(dp[i-1][j], dp[i][j-1], dp[i-1][j-1]) + 1;
                maxSide = Math.max(maxSide, dp[i][j]);
            }
    return maxSide * maxSide;
}

// Maximal Rectangle
function maximalRectangle(matrix) {
    const m = matrix.length, n = matrix[0].length;
    const heights = new Array(n).fill(0);
    let maxArea = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        for (let j = 0; j < n; j++)
            heights[j] = matrix[i][j] === '1' ? heights[j] + 1 : 0;
        const stack = [-1];
        for (let j = 0; j <= n; j++) {
            const h = j === n ? 0 : heights[j];
            while (stack.length > 1 && heights[stack[stack.length-1]] > h) {
                const height = heights[stack.pop()];
                const width = j - stack[stack.length-1] - 1;
                maxArea = Math.max(maxArea, height * width);
            }
            stack.push(j);
        }
    }
    return maxArea;
}

// Largest Rectangle in Histogram
function largestRectangleArea(heights) {
    const stack = [-1];
    let maxArea = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i <= heights.length; i++) {
        const h = i === heights.length ? 0 : heights[i];
        while (stack.length > 1 && heights[stack[stack.length-1]] > h) {
            const height = heights[stack.pop()];
            const width = i - stack[stack.length-1] - 1;
            maxArea = Math.max(maxArea, height * width);
        }
        stack.push(i);
    }
    return maxArea;
}

// Count Squares in Matrix
function countSquares(matrix) {
    const m = matrix.length, n = matrix[0].length;
    const dp = Array(m+1).fill(0).map(() => Array(n+1).fill(0));
    let total = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i <= m; i++)
        for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++)
            if (matrix[i-1][j-1] === 1) {
                dp[i][j] = Math.min(dp[i-1][j], dp[i][j-1], dp[i-1][j-1]) + 1;
                total += dp[i][j];
            }
    return total;
}
